===== FILE packages/cli/src/engineering-command.ts sha256=e0a6066201e728f0fc7faa9ba9876e3f7213af8c43420b6c99a851a671bd7646 =====
1: import { closeSync, constants, fstatSync, openSync, readSync } from "node:fs";
2: import {
3:   EngineeringAdoptRequest, EngineeringObserveRequest, EngineeringDecideRequest,
4:   EngineeringDeferRequest, EngineeringCancelRequest, EngineeringEndObservationRequest,
5:   EngineeringReconcileRequest,
6: } from "@osstack/protocol";
7: 
8: const HELP = `Usage: osstack engineering <command> [flags] [--json]
9: Session adoption pilot. Completion level: observed_turn (the reply turn, not code delivery).
10:   adopt --scope ID --task ID --run ID --session ID --goal TEXT --request ID
11:   observe --mission ID [--cursor N] [--limit 1..100]
12:   decide --observation FILE --wait ID --action ID (--choice ID | --text TEXT)
13:   defer --observation FILE --wait ID --request ID --revisit-at UTC_ISO --reason TEXT
14:   cancel --mission ID --revision N --request ID [--reason TEXT]
15:   end-observation --mission ID --revision N --request ID [--reason TEXT]
16:   reconcile --kind query_action --mission ID --action ID
17:   reconcile --kind dispose_wait --observation FILE --wait ID --request ID
18:     --disposition human_answered_out_of_band|no_longer_relevant --reason TEXT
19: Each command also accepts --input FILE instead of request flags (strict JSON, at most 1 MiB).
20: Save observe --json output to use --observation. Saved revisions remain exact; they are not refreshed.
21: Use the same action/request ID and unchanged input when retrying an uncertain request.
22: Host setup: OSSTACK_ENGINEERING=1 and OSSTACK_ATTENTION=1. No daemon starts automatically.
23: The host assigns identity and rights through OSSTACK_ENGINEERING_CREDENTIAL. No --actor flag.
24: Cancel requests a stop. End-observation does not stop the Worker or claim completion.
25: See docs/guides/engineering-session-adoption.md (中文) and engineering-session-adoption.en.md.`;
26: 
27: const requests = {
28:   adopt: EngineeringAdoptRequest,
29:   observe: EngineeringObserveRequest,
30:   decide: EngineeringDecideRequest,
31:   defer: EngineeringDeferRequest,
32:   cancel: EngineeringCancelRequest,
33:   "end-observation": EngineeringEndObservationRequest,
34:   reconcile: EngineeringReconcileRequest,
35: } as const;
36: type Command = keyof typeof requests;
37: 
38: export interface EngineeringCommandDeps {
39:   stdout?: (line: string) => void;
40:   stderr?: (line: string) => void;
41:   rpc?: (method: string, params?: unknown, timeoutMs?: number) => Promise<unknown>;
42:   readCredential?: () => string;
43:   readInput?: (path: string) => Promise<unknown>;
44: }
45: 
46: function safeJson(value: unknown): string {
47:   return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2).replace(/[\u007f-\u009f\p{Cf}]/gu, (char) =>
48:     char.split("").map((unit) => `\\u${unit.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).padStart(4, "0")}`).join(""));
49: }
50: function object(value: unknown, label: string): Record<string, unknown> {
51:   if (value === null || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`invalid_${label}`);
52:   return value as Record<string, unknown>;
53: }
54: async function readBoundedJson(path: string): Promise<unknown> {
55:   const fd = openSync(path, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW);
56:   try {
57:     const limit = 1024 * 1024;
58:     const stat = fstatSync(fd);
59:     if (!stat.isFile() || stat.size > limit) throw new Error("input_file_must_be_regular_and_at_most_1MiB");
60:     const buffer = Buffer.alloc(limit + 1);
61:     let length = 0;
62:     while (length < buffer.length) {
63:       const count = readSync(fd, buffer, length, buffer.length - length, null);
64:       if (!count) break;
65:       length += count;
66:     }
67:     if (length > limit) throw new Error("input_file_exceeds_1MiB");
68:     return JSON.parse(buffer.subarray(0, length).toString("utf8")) as unknown;
69:   } finally { closeSync(fd); }
70: }
71: 
72: const flagsByCommand: Record<Command, readonly string[]> = {
73:   adopt: ["scope", "task", "run", "session", "goal", "request"],
74:   observe: ["mission", "cursor", "limit"],
75:   decide: ["observation", "wait", "action", "choice", "text"],
76:   defer: ["observation", "wait", "request", "revisit-at", "reason"],
77:   cancel: ["mission", "revision", "request", "reason"],
78:   "end-observation": ["mission", "revision", "request", "reason"],
79:   reconcile: ["kind", "mission", "action", "observation", "wait", "request", "disposition", "reason"],
80: };
81: 
82: export async function handleEngineeringCommand(args: string[], deps: EngineeringCommandDeps = {}): Promise<number> {
83:   const out = deps.stdout ?? console.log;
84:   const err = deps.stderr ?? console.error;
85:   let credential = "";
86:   if (args.length === 0 || ["help", "--help", "-h"].includes(args[0]!)) {
87:     out(HELP);
88:     return 0;
89:   }
90:   try {
91:     const commandName = args[0]!;
92:     if (!Object.hasOwn(requests, commandName)) throw new Error("unknown_engineering_command");
93:     const command = commandName as Command;
94:     const flags = new Map<string, string>();
95:     let json = false;
96:     for (let i = 1; i < args.length; i += 1) {
97:       const arg = args[i]!;
98:       if (arg === "--help" || arg === "-h") { out(HELP); return 0; }
99:       if (!arg.startsWith("--")) throw new Error(`unexpected_argument:${arg}`);
100:       const key = arg.slice(2);
101:       if (flags.has(key) || (key === "json" && json)) throw new Error(`duplicate_flag:--${key}`);
102:       if (key === "json") { json = true; continue; }
103:       if (key !== "input" && !flagsByCommand[command].includes(key)) throw new Error(`unknown_flag:--${key}`);
104:       const value = args[++i];
105:       if (value === undefined || value.startsWith("--") || value.includes("\0")) throw new Error(`missing_value:--${key}`);
106:       flags.set(key, value);
107:     }
108:     const required = (key: string): string => {
109:       const value = flags.get(key);
110:       if (value === undefined || value.length === 0) throw new Error(`required:--${key}`);
111:       return value;
112:     };
113:     const readInput = deps.readInput ?? readBoundedJson;
114:     let input: unknown;
115:     if (flags.has("input")) {
116:       if (flags.size !== 1) throw new Error("input_cannot_be_combined_with_request_flags");
117:       input = await readInput(required("input"));
118:     } else if (command === "adopt") {
119:       input = { scope_id: required("scope"), task_id: required("task"), run_id: required("run"), session_id: required("session"), goal: required("goal"), request_id: required("request") };
120:     } else if (command === "observe") {
121:       input = { mission_id: required("mission"), ...(flags.has("cursor") ? { cursor: Number(required("cursor")) } : {}), ...(flags.has("limit") ? { limit: Number(required("limit")) } : {}) };
122:     } else if (command === "cancel" || command === "end-observation") {
123:       input = { mission_id: required("mission"), if_revision: Number(required("revision")), request_id: required("request"), ...(flags.has("reason") ? { reason: required("reason") } : {}) };
124:     } else if (command === "reconcile" && required("kind") === "query_action") {
125:       for (const key of flags.keys()) if (!["kind", "mission", "action"].includes(key)) throw new Error(`unexpected_query_action_flag:--${key}`);
126:       input = { kind: "query_action", mission_id: required("mission"), action_id: required("action") };
127:     } else {
128:       if (command === "reconcile") {
129:         if (required("kind") !== "dispose_wait") throw new Error("unknown_reconciliation_kind");
130:         if (flags.has("mission") || flags.has("action")) throw new Error("dispose_wait_requires_frozen_observation");
131:       }
132:       const view = object(await readInput(required("observation")), "observation");
133:       const mission = object(view["mission"], "observation_mission");
134:       if (!Array.isArray(view["waits"])) throw new Error("invalid_observation_waits");
135:       const matches = view["waits"].map((wait: unknown) => object(wait, "wait")).filter((wait: Record<string, unknown>) => wait["wait_id"] === required("wait"));
136:       if (matches.length !== 1) throw new Error("wait_not_unique_in_observation");
137:       const wait = matches[0]!;
138:       const common = { mission_id: mission["mission_id"], wait_id: wait["wait_id"], if_revision: mission["revision"] };
139:       if (command === "decide") {
140:         if (Number(flags.has("choice")) + Number(flags.has("text")) !== 1) throw new Error("exactly_one_of_choice_or_text_required");
141:         input = { ...common, decision_ref: wait["decision_ref"], action_id: required("action"), interaction_token: wait["interaction_token"], answer: flags.has("choice") ? { kind: "choice", choice_id: required("choice") } : { kind: "text", text: required("text") } };
142:       } else if (command === "defer") {
143:         input = { ...common, wait_revision: wait["revision"], request_id: required("request"), revisit_at: required("revisit-at"), reason: required("reason") };
144:       } else {
145:         input = { kind: "dispose_wait", ...common, wait_revision: wait["revision"], request_id: required("request"), observed_source_version: view["source_version"], observed_turn_id: view["turn_id"], observed_snapshot_digest: view["snapshot_digest"], disposition: required("disposition"), reason: required("reason") };
146:       }
147:     }
148:     const parsed = requests[command].safeParse(input);
149:     if (!parsed.success) throw new Error(`invalid_request:${parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => `${issue.path.join(".")}:${issue.code}`).join(",")}`);
150:     if (!deps.rpc) throw new Error("daemon_connection_required");
151:     credential = deps.readCredential ? deps.readCredential() : (await import("./engineering-access.js")).readEngineeringCredential();
152:     // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noControlCharactersInRegex: reject bearer transport control bytes before RPC.
153:     if (credential.length < 32 || credential.length > 4096 || /[\u0000\r\n]/u.test(credential)) throw new Error("engineering_credential_required");
154:     const method = command === "end-observation" ? "engineering.endObservation" : `engineering.${command}`;
155:     const response = object(await deps.rpc(method, { ...parsed.data, credential }, 120000), "daemon_response");
156:     if (response["ok"] !== true) {
157:       const error = object(response["error"] ?? {}, "daemon_error");
158:       throw new Error(typeof error["code"] === "string" ? error["code"] : "engineering_request_failed");
159:     }
160:     const text = safeJson(response["result"] ?? null).split(credential).join("[REDACTED]");
161:     out(json ? text : `Engineering · ${command}\n${text}\nobserved_turn confirms one reply turn. It does not prove code delivery. Stop requests remain unconfirmed until host evidence exists.`);
162:     return 0;
163:   } catch (error) {
164:     const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
165:     const redacted = credential ? message.split(credential).join("[REDACTED]") : message;
166:     err(`osstack engineering: ${safeJson(redacted)}`);
167:     return 1;
168:   }
169: }

50: async function rpc(root: string, method: string, params: unknown): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
51:   return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
52:     const socket = connect(join(root, "d.sock")); const decoder = new FrameDecoder();
53:     const timer = setTimeout(() => { socket.destroy(); reject(Error("RPC timeout")); }, 5000);
54:     socket.on("connect", () => socket.write(encodeFrame({ id: 1, method, params })));
55:     socket.on("error", error => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(error); });
56:     socket.on("data", chunk => { const response = decoder.push(chunk)[0] as { ok: boolean; result: Record<string, unknown>; error?: unknown } | undefined;
57:       if (response) { clearTimeout(timer); socket.end(); if (response.ok) resolve(response.result); else reject(Error(JSON.stringify(response.error))); }
58:     });
59:   });
60: }

13:   active(sessionId: string): boolean;
14:   acquire(session: Session, messageId: string): Promise<Admission>;
15:   execute(dispatch: Dispatch, session: Session, admission: Admission): Promise<void>;
16:   stop(input: { runId: string; sessionId: string; messageIds: readonly string[] }): boolean;
17: }
18: 
19: /** No second sender/outbox. This host only drives first dispatch of the existing receipt. */
20: export class EngineeringHost {
21:   private readonly pending = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
22:   private closed = false;
23:   private readonly blocked = new Map<string, { attempts: number; nextAt: number }>();
24:   private timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
25:   private cursor: { created_at: number; action_id: string } | undefined;
26:   private missionCursor: { created_at: number; mission_id: string } | undefined;
27:   constructor(readonly store: EngineeringStore, readonly access: EngineeringAccess, private readonly ports: EngineeringHostPorts) {}
28: 
29:   async handle(method: string, params: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
30:     if (this.closed) throw new Error("engineering_unavailable");
31:     if (!(method in EngineeringRpcRequests)) throw new Error("unknown_method");
32:     const schema = EngineeringRpcRequests[method as keyof typeof EngineeringRpcRequests];
33:     const parsed = schema.safeParse(params);
34:     if (!parsed.success) throw new Error("bad_request");
35:     const { credential, ...input } = parsed.data;
36:     const actor = this.access.authenticate(credential);
37:     // Reparse under the concrete schema below: the wire union cannot infer the method correlation.
38:     if (method === "engineering.adopt") {
39:       if (this.ports.draining()) throw new Error("draining");
40:       const p = EngineeringRpcRequests["engineering.adopt"].parse(params);
41:       if (!actor.read_scopes.includes(p.scope_id)) throw new Error("permission_denied");
42:       const scope = this.store.attention.getScope(p.scope_id);
43:       const session = this.ports.getSession(p.session_id);
44:       if (!scope || scope.task_id !== p.task_id || !session || session.task_id !== scope.task_id || session.run_id !== p.run_id) throw new Error("permission_denied");
45:       if (!this.ports.qualified(session)) throw new Error("executor_not_qualified");
46:       this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
47:       const { credential: _credential, ...request } = p;
48:       return this.store.adopt(actor, request);
49:     }
50:     if (!("mission_id" in input)) throw new Error("bad_request");
51:     const mission = this.store.readableMission(actor, input.mission_id);
52:     this.access.assertOutsideCanonicalRepository(mission.repo_path);
53:     switch (method) {
54:       case "engineering.observe": {
55:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
56:         return this.store.observe(actor, p);
57:       }
58:       case "engineering.decide": {
59:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
60:         // Read-only receipt replay is allowed while drained or at capacity. The
61:         // domain still verifies actor/scope and the complete immutable payload.
62:         const replay = this.store.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_requests WHERE principal_id=? AND client_session_id=? AND request_id=? AND operation='decide'").get(actor.principal_id, actor.client_session_id, p.action_id);
63:         if (!replay && this.ports.draining()) throw new Error("draining");
64:         if (!replay && this.pending.size >= 16) throw new Error("engineering_busy");
65:         const result = this.store.decide(actor, p);
66:         if (!result.replay) {
67:           const phase = this.store.getDispatch(result.message_id);
68:           if (phase) this.schedule(phase.action_id);
69:         }
70:         return result;
71:       }
72:       case "engineering.defer": {
73:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
74:         return this.store.defer(actor, p);
75:       }
76:       case "engineering.cancel": {
77:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
78:         const result = this.store.cancel(actor, p);
79:         if (!result.replay) {
80:           const requested = this.ports.stop({ runId: result.run_id, sessionId: result.session_id,
81:             messageIds: [...result.committed_message_ids, ...this.cancelledAdmissions(result.mission.mission_id, result.action_ids)] });
82:           this.store.recordStopReceipt(result.mission.mission_id, result.stop_intent_id, "stop_unknown", {
83:             abort_requested: requested,
84:             reason: requested ? "abort is a request; containment exit is not proven" : "no live invocation in this daemon; prior worker exit is unknown",
85:           });
86:         }
87:         return result;
88:       }
89:       case "engineering.endObservation": {
90:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
91:         const result = this.store.endObservation(actor, p);
92:         if (!result.replay) {
93:           // Ending observation may release only an original queue reservation
94:           // proven never dispatched. It must not stop a committed Worker.
95:           const messages = this.cancelledAdmissions(result.mission.mission_id, result.action_ids);
96:           if (messages.length) this.ports.stop({ runId: result.run_id, sessionId: result.session_id, messageIds: messages });
97:         }
98:         return result;
99:       }
100:       case "engineering.reconcile": {
101:         const { credential: _credential, ...p } = EngineeringRpcRequests[method].parse(params);
102:         return this.store.reconcile(actor, p);
103:       }
104:       default: throw new Error("unknown_method");
105:     }
106:   }
107: 
108:   private cancelledAdmissions(missionId: string, actionIds: readonly string[]): string[] {
109:     const messages: string[] = [];
110:     for (const actionId of actionIds) {
111:       const phase = this.store.db.prepare("SELECT message_id FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE mission_id=? AND action_id=? AND phase='cancelled_before_dispatch'").get(missionId, actionId) as { message_id: string } | undefined;
112:       if (phase && isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.store.db, phase.message_id)) messages.push(phase.message_id);
113:     }
114:     return messages;
115:   }
116:   /** No model polling. Bounded pages let a denied old action coexist with later work. */
117:   start(): void {
118:     if (this.closed || this.timer) return;
119:     this.tick();
120:     this.timer = setInterval(() => this.tick(), 1000);
121:     this.timer.unref();
122:   }
123:   private tick(): void {
124:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
125:     this.recover();
126:     try {
127:       const rows = this.store.listActiveMissions(16, this.missionCursor);
128:       for (const row of rows) {
129:         try { this.store.refresh(row.mission_id); } catch { /* Retain original source obligations. */ }
130:       }
131:       const last = rows.at(-1);
132:       this.missionCursor = rows.length === 16 && last ? { created_at: last.created_at, mission_id: last.mission_id } : undefined;
133:     } catch { /* Fencing failures cannot become observer or dispatch authority. */ }
134:   }
135:   /** Only persisted reserved phases are eligible, never committed/legacy. */
136:   recover(): void {
137:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
138:     try {
139:       const rows = this.store.listReserved(16, this.cursor);
140:       for (const row of rows) this.schedule(row.action_id);
141:       const last = rows.at(-1);
142:       this.cursor = rows.length === 16 && last ? { created_at: last.created_at, action_id: last.action_id } : undefined;
143:     } catch { /* A stale owner or unavailable database cannot authorize dispatch. */ }
144:   }
145:   private schedule(actionId: string): void {
146:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining() || this.pending.has(actionId) || this.pending.size >= 16 || (this.blocked.get(actionId)?.nextAt ?? 0) > Date.now()) return;
147:     // Let the durable received response become observable before expensive admission.
148:     const pending = new Promise<void>(resolve => setImmediate(resolve))
149:       .then(() => this.dispatch(actionId))
150:       .catch(error => {
151:         const reason = error instanceof Error && /^[a-z_]{1,64}$/.test(error.message) ? error.message : "admission_denied";
152:         const attempts = Math.min((this.blocked.get(actionId)?.attempts ?? 0) + 1, 7);
153:         this.blocked.set(actionId, { attempts, nextAt: Date.now() + Math.min(60_000, 1000 * 2 ** (attempts - 1)) });
154:         // The durable store deduplicates each reason. A denied old owner must
155:         // neither spin on writer admission nor disappear behind a swallowed error.
156:         try { this.store.recordDispatchBlocked(actionId, reason); } catch { /* A stale owner cannot write diagnostics. */ }
157:       })
158:       .finally(() => this.pending.delete(actionId));
159:     this.pending.set(actionId, pending);
160:   }
161:   private async dispatch(actionId: string): Promise<void> {
162:     if (this.closed || this.ports.draining()) return;
163:     const row = this.store.getReserved(actionId);
164:     if (!row) { this.blocked.delete(actionId); return; }
165:     // Cheap read-only rejection comes before any health probe, semaphore or
166:     // repository writer lock. The final post-admission checks still run below.
167:     const preliminaryActor = this.access.refresh(row.owner_principal_id, row.client_session_id);
168:     this.store.preflightDispatch(actionId, preliminaryActor);
169:     const session = this.ports.getSession(row.session_id);
170:     if (!session || !this.ports.qualified(session)) throw new Error("executor_not_qualified");
171:     if (this.ports.active(session.session_id)) throw new Error("session_busy");
172:     this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
173:     const admission = await this.ports.acquire(session, row.message_id);
174:     let handedOff = false;
175:     try {
176:       if (this.closed || this.ports.draining() || admission.controller.signal.aborted) return;
177:       // The latest authority is read after asynchronous admission, immediately before synchronous CAS.
178:       const actor = this.access.refresh(row.owner_principal_id, row.client_session_id);
179:       this.access.assertOutsideRepository(session.repo_path);
180:       const dispatch = this.store.commitDispatch(actionId, actor);
181:       this.blocked.delete(actionId);
182:       handedOff = true;
183:       // A pilot reply turn has a host-enforced five-minute wall-clock budget.
184:       // Abort requests cancellation; the existing helper retains admission if
185:       // actual adapter exit remains unproved.
186:       const deadline = setTimeout(() => admission.controller.abort(), 300_000);
187:       deadline.unref();
188:       try { await this.ports.execute(dispatch, session, admission); }
189:       finally { clearTimeout(deadline); }
190:     } finally { if (!handedOff) await admission.release(); }
191:   }
192:   async close(): Promise<void> {
193:     this.closed = true;
194:     if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
195:     this.timer = undefined;
196:     await Promise.allSettled(this.pending.values());
197:   }
198: }

===== FILE packages/cli/src/engineering-upgrade-guard.ts sha256=909b03a706135e0b70904063d82938e3c1f7b4e783f68e0cc2a1af92eef2c7df =====
1: import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
2: import { isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch } from "@osstack/core";
3: 
4: /** Pure read used before stopping a daemon and again under the stopped-home
5:  * database lock. Only proven completion or proven non-dispatch clears a hold. */
6: export function engineeringUpgradeHolds(db: Database.Database): number {
7:   if (!db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='attention_dispatch_phases'").get()) return 0;
8:   const rows = db.prepare(`SELECT p.message_id,p.phase,m.state
9:     FROM attention_dispatch_phases p LEFT JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=p.message_id`).all() as Array<{
10:     message_id: string; phase: string; state: string | null;
11:   }>;
12:   let holds = 0;
13:   for (const row of rows) {
14:     if (row.phase === "dispatch_committed" && row.state === "applied") continue;
15:     if (row.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") {
16:       try { if (isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db, row.message_id)) continue; }
17:       catch { /* Missing or malformed proof dependencies remain held. */ }
18:     }
19:     holds++;
20:   }
21:   return holds;
22: }

===== FILE packages/cli/src/governed-execution.ts sha256=471d5ba2b17392ce4dce2b31df64673f71bac02b5b5a5ae58d7ef4e703c93f75 =====
1: import { join } from "node:path";
2: import { acquireRepoWriteLock, FifoSemaphore, SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError, type SemaphoreLease, type RepoWriteLock } from '@osstack/autonomy';
3: import { resolveRepositoryScope } from '@osstack/core';
4: 
5: export interface ExecutionLease {
6:   /** Idempotent after physical release, including retries of a lost DB acknowledgement. */
7:   release(): Promise<void>;
8:   /** Keep ownership until a trusted caller has confirmed external exit. */
9:   retain(reason: string): void;
10:   /** Trusted host only; never accept a worker-provided proof callback.
11:    * Idempotent after release: recovery must never release semaphore permits twice. */
12:   releaseAfterReconciliation?(verify: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void>;
13:   /** Assert this exact canonical writer lease is still live. */
14:   assertActive?(): Promise<void>;
15:   /** Scoped synchronous file operations under the canonical writer lock. This
16:    * capability is absent on read leases. Failures retain admission until recovery. */
17:   withWriteGuard?<T>(workspacePath: string, operation: (assertActive: () => void) => T): Promise<T>;
18: }
19: export interface AdmissionInput {
20:   kind: 'read' | 'write';
21:   runId: string;
22:   /** Supplied by the daemon registration, never by an adapter context. */
23:   workspacePath?: string;
24:   repoId?: string;
25:   signal?: AbortSignal;
26:   authorize?: () => Promise<void>;
27:   /** Host-only synchronous proof: acquisition rejected with no lease or cleanup obligation. */
28:   onNotAcquired?: () => void;
29: }
30: export class GovernedExecution {
31:   private readonly activeRuns = new Set<string>();
32:   private readonly quarantinedRepositories = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
33:   private quarantine(repoId: string, runId: string): void {
34:     const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(repoId) ?? new Set<string>();
35:     owners.add(runId); this.quarantinedRepositories.set(repoId, owners);
36:   }
37:   constructor(private readonly options: {
38:     reads: FifoSemaphore;
39:     writes: FifoSemaphore;
40:     repositories: Map<string, FifoSemaphore>;
41:     checkHealth: () => Promise<void>;
42:   }) {}
43: 
44:   get hasWriters(): boolean { return this.options.writes.in_use > 0; }
45: 
46:   async acquire(input: AdmissionInput): Promise<ExecutionLease> {
47:     const cleanup = { confirmed: true };
48:     try { return await this.acquireOwned(input, cleanup); }
49:     catch (error) {
50:       if (cleanup.confirmed && !this.activeRuns.has(input.runId)) input.onNotAcquired?.();
51:       throw error;
52:     }
53:   }
54: 
55:   private async acquireOwned(input: AdmissionInput, cleanup: { confirmed: boolean }): Promise<ExecutionLease> {
56:     const check = async (): Promise<void> => {
57:       if (input.signal?.aborted) throw new SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError();
58:       if (input.kind === 'write') await this.options.checkHealth();
59:       await input.authorize?.();
60:     };
61:     await check();
62:     const scope = input.workspacePath === undefined ? undefined
63:       : await resolveRepositoryScope(input.workspacePath, input.repoId === undefined ? {} : { repoId: input.repoId });
64:     if (input.kind === 'write' && !scope) throw new Error('trusted repository scope required for writes');
65:     if (input.kind === "write" && scope && this.quarantinedRepositories.has(scope.repoId)) throw new Error("repository external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required");
66:     if (this.activeRuns.has(input.runId)) {
67:       cleanup.confirmed = false;
68:       throw new Error("run already admitted or queued");
69:     }
70:     this.activeRuns.add(input.runId);
71:     let local: SemaphoreLease | undefined;
72:     let global: SemaphoreLease | undefined;
73:     let lock: RepoWriteLock | undefined;
74:     let queue: FifoSemaphore | undefined;
75:     const releasePermits = (): void => {
76:       global?.release(); local?.release();
77:       this.activeRuns.delete(input.runId);
78:       if (scope && queue?.in_use === 0 && queue.pending === 0) this.options.repositories.delete(scope.repoId);
79:     };
80:     let released = false;
81:     let retained = false;
82:     let writeGuardActive = false;
83:     // Start as soon as the filesystem lock exists, including the final policy
84:     // wait. Once admitted, a renewal failure retains the unknown producer.
85:     let renewing: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
86:     let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
87:     const startRenewal = () => {
88:       timer = setInterval(() => {
89:         if (writeGuardActive) return;
90:         renewing = renewing.then(async () => {
91:           if (!released && !writeGuardActive) await lock!.renew();
92:         }).catch((error: unknown) => {
93:           retained = true;
94:           if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
95:           process.stderr.write(`repository lease renewal failed; retained ${input.runId}: ${String(error)}\n`);
96:           if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
97:         });
98:       }, 20_000);
99:       timer.unref();
100:     };
101:     try {
102:       if (input.kind === 'write' && scope) {
103:         queue = this.options.repositories.get(scope.repoId) ?? new FifoSemaphore(1);
104:         this.options.repositories.set(scope.repoId, queue);
105:         local = await queue.acquire(input.signal);
106:       }
107:       global = await (input.kind === 'read' ? this.options.reads : this.options.writes).acquire(input.signal);
108:       if (input.kind === 'write' && scope) {
109:         // A failed filesystem acquisition may have left an ownership obligation.
110:         cleanup.confirmed = false;
111:         lock = await acquireRepoWriteLock({ root_dir: join(scope.commonDir, "osstack", "writer-locks"), recover_stale: false, repo_id: scope.repoId, autonomy_run_id: input.runId });
112:         // Authorization may wait behind another branch's synchronous work.
113:         // Start ownership renewal before that wait, not after publishing admission.
114:         startRenewal();
115:       }
116:       if (input.kind === "write" && scope && this.quarantinedRepositories.has(scope.repoId)) throw new Error("repository external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required");
117:       await check();
118:       await renewing;
119:       if (retained) throw new Error("repository lease renewal failed during admission");
120:       await lock?.assertActive();
121:       if (retained) throw new Error("repository lease renewal failed during admission");
122:       if (input.signal?.aborted) throw new SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError();
123:     } catch (error) {
124:       if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
125:       await renewing;
126:       try {
127:         await lock?.release();
128:         if (lock) cleanup.confirmed = true;
129:         // No executor was admitted. Remove only this rejected acquisition's quarantine.
130:         if (scope) {
131:           const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(scope.repoId);
132:           owners?.delete(input.runId);
133:           if (owners?.size === 0) this.quarantinedRepositories.delete(scope.repoId);
134:         }
135:       } catch (releaseError) {
136:         if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
137:         throw new AggregateError([error, releaseError], 'admission rejected and ownership cleanup failed');
138:       } finally { releasePermits(); }
139:       throw error;
140:     }
141:     let reconciled: Promise<void> | undefined;
142:     let releasing: Promise<void> | undefined;
143:     return {
144:       ...(lock && scope ? { withWriteGuard: async <T>(workspacePath: string, operation: (assertActive: () => void) => T): Promise<T> => {
145:         if (workspacePath !== scope.workspacePath) throw new Error('write guard workspace mismatch');
146:         if (released || retained || writeGuardActive || reconciled || releasing) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
147:         writeGuardActive = true;
148:         try {
149:           await renewing;
150:           if (released || retained || releasing) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
151:           // Do not let the timer contend with our metadata guard. Renew on both
152:           // sides while still excluding release; renewal never revives expiry.
153:           await lock!.renew();
154:           const value = await lock!.withActiveGuard(check => {
155:             const assertActive = () => {
156:               if (released || retained || releasing) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
157:               check();
158:             };
159:             assertActive(); const result = operation(assertActive);
160:             if (result !== null && (typeof result === 'object' || typeof result === 'function') && 'then' in result) {
161:               void Promise.resolve(result).catch(() => {});
162:               throw new Error('write guard operation must be synchronous');
163:             }
164:             assertActive(); return result;
165:           });
166:           await lock!.renew();
167:           return value;
168:         } catch (error) {
169:           retained = true; if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
170:           this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
171:           throw error;
172:         } finally { writeGuardActive = false; }
173:       } } : {}),
174:       assertActive: async () => {
175:         if (released || retained || releasing) throw new Error("execution lease unavailable");
176:         await lock?.assertActive();
177:         if (released || retained || releasing) throw new Error("execution lease unavailable");
178:       },
179:       releaseAfterReconciliation: (verify) => {
180:         if (typeof verify !== 'function') return Promise.reject(new Error('trusted reconciliation verifier required'));
181:         if (releasing || released || reconciled) {
182:           const pending = releasing ?? reconciled;
183:           return (async () => {
184:             const result: unknown = await verify();
185:             if (result !== undefined) throw new Error('invalid reconciliation authority');
186:             await pending;
187:           })();
188:         }
189:         if (writeGuardActive) return Promise.reject(new Error('write guard in progress'));
190:         reconciled = (async () => {
191:           // Stop renewal first. No authority is freed until the verifier succeeds
192:           // and the canonical token is released under its filesystem guard.
193:           if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
194:           await renewing;
195:           const result: unknown = await verify();
196:           if (result !== undefined) throw new Error('invalid reconciliation authority');
197:           await lock?.release();
198:           released = true; retained = false;
199:           if (scope) {
200:             const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(scope.repoId);
201:             owners?.delete(input.runId);
202:             if (owners?.size === 0) this.quarantinedRepositories.delete(scope.repoId);
203:           }
204:           releasePermits();
205:         })().catch((error: unknown) => {
206:           retained = true;
207:           if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
208:           throw error;
209:         }).finally(() => { reconciled = undefined; });
210:         return reconciled;
211:       },
212:       retain: (reason) => { retained = true; if (timer) clearInterval(timer); if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId); process.stderr.write(`osstack: retaining write lease ${input.runId}: ${reason}\n`); },
213:       release: () => {
214:         if (releasing) return releasing;
215:         if (reconciled) return reconciled;
216:         if (writeGuardActive) return Promise.reject(new Error('write guard in progress'));
217:         if (released) return Promise.resolve();
218:         if (retained) return Promise.reject(new Error("external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required before release"));
219:         releasing = (async () => {
220:           if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
221:           await renewing;
222:           if (retained) throw new Error("external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required before release");
223:           // Physical completion, not starting its asynchronous release, is the
224:           // final state. Concurrent release/reconciliation callers share this wait.
225:           try { await lock?.release(); }
226:           catch (error) {
227:             retained = true;
228:             if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
229:             process.stderr.write(`repository lock release failed; retained ${input.runId}: ${String(error)}\n`);
230:             throw error;
231:           }
232:           released = true;
233:           releasePermits();
234:         })().finally(() => { releasing = undefined; });
235:         return releasing;
236:       },
237:     };
238:   }
239: }

13: type LoopReadInput = { repo: string; path: string; loop_id: string; signal: AbortSignal };
14: export interface SupervisionOptions {
15:   loopReader?: (input: LoopReadInput) => Promise<unknown>;
16:   readTimeoutMs?: number;
17: }
18: type ReadFailure = "timeout" | "aborted" | "invalid_payload" | "read_failed" | "read_capacity_exceeded";
19: type LoopFacts = Record<string, unknown> & { state: string; reason?: string };
20: interface ReadSlot {
21:   controller: AbortController;
22:   logical: Promise<LoopFacts>;
23: }
24: interface LoopBinding {
25:   binding_hash: string;
26:   admitted: number;
27:   generation: number;
28:   payload_json: string;
29: }
30: function loopPayload(json: string): Record<string, unknown> | null {
31:   try {
32:     const value: unknown = JSON.parse(json);
33:     return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
34:       ? value as Record<string, unknown> : null;
35:   } catch { return null; }
36: }
37: function validLoopFacts(value: unknown, id: string): value is LoopFacts {
38:   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
39:   const v = value as Record<string, unknown>;
40:   if (v.loop_id !== id || typeof v.path !== "string" || v.process_liveness !== "unknown") return false;
41:   if (v.state === "source_missing") return v.coverage === "uncovered" && v.gap === "source_missing";
42:   return v.state === "snapshot_available" && v.coverage === "snapshot_only"
43:     && v.gap === "best_effort_snapshot_no_event_replay" && typeof v.updated_at === "string"
44:     && Array.isArray(v.in_progress) && Array.isArray(v.pending)
45:     && Number.isSafeInteger(v.completed_count) && (v.completed_count as number) >= 0
46:     && typeof v.recovery_required === "boolean";
47: }
48: /** The daemon has already acquired its kernel process lock before construction. */
49: export class SupervisionService {
50:   readonly store: AttentionStore;
51:   readonly observer: AttentionObserver;
52:   readonly experience: AttentionExperience;
53:   private delivering = false;
54:   private deliveryRecoveryNeeded = false;
55:   private readonly deliveryAbort = new AbortController();
56:   private deliveryDone: Promise<void> | undefined;
57:   private ciReader: import("@osstack/github").AttentionCiReader | undefined;
58:   private timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
59:   private started = false;
60:   private stopped = false;
61:   private dirty = false;
62:   private scanDone: Promise<void> | undefined;
63:   private stopDone: Promise<void> | undefined;
64:   private epoch = 0;
65:   // A slot outlives its logical timeout until the underlying promise settles.
66:   // It holds no continuation that can access SQLite after stop.
67:   private readonly readers = new Map<string, ReadSlot>();
68:   private readonly logicalReads = new Set<Promise<LoopFacts>>();
69:   private readonly readTimeoutMs: number;
70:   constructor(
71:     db: Database.Database,
72:     path: string,
73:     readonly sender?: AttentionSender,
74:     private readonly options: SupervisionOptions = {}
75:   ) {
76:     this.readTimeoutMs = options.readTimeoutMs ?? 1000;
77:     if (!Number.isFinite(this.readTimeoutMs) || this.readTimeoutMs < 1 || this.readTimeoutMs > 1000)
78:       throw new Error("invalid_read_timeout");
79:     this.store = new AttentionStore(db, path);
80:     this.observer = new AttentionObserver(
81:       this.store,
82:       this.store.claimOwner(randomUUID())
83:     );
84:     this.experience = new AttentionExperience(this.store, this.observer.owner);
85:     this.experience.recoverDeliveries();
86:     new AttentionActions(this.store, this.observer.owner).recover();
87:   }
88:   private assertRunning(): void {
89:     if (this.stopped) throw new Error("supervision_stopped");
90:   }
91:   start(): void {
92:     this.assertRunning();
93:     if (this.started) return;
94:     this.started = true;
95:     void this.requestScan("start").catch(() => {});
96:     this.timer = setInterval(() => { void this.requestScan("timer").catch(() => {}); }, 30000);
97:     this.timer.unref();
98:   }
99:   /** Dirty signals are hints; each pass still reads the persisted source facts. */
100:   requestScan(_reason?: string): Promise<void> {
101:     if (this.stopped) return Promise.reject(new Error("supervision_stopped"));
102:     this.dirty = true;
103:     this.scanDone ??= Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
104:       while (this.dirty && !this.stopped) {
105:         this.dirty = false;
106:         try { await this.scan(); } catch { /* prior coverage expires */ }
107:       }
108:     }).finally(() => {
109:       this.scanDone = undefined;
110:       // A dirty signal can arrive after the loop exits but before this cleanup.
111:       if (this.dirty && !this.stopped) return this.requestScan("coalesced");
112:       return undefined;
113:     });
114:     return this.scanDone;
115:   }
116:   private async scan(): Promise<void> {
117:     this.assertRunning();
118:     while (!this.stopped) {
119:       // A socket request spans multiple event-loop turns (accept, frame, reply).
120:       // Leave room within its 10ms p95 budget instead of consuming the observer's
121:       // entire 5ms ceiling on every turn. The observer still checks each subject.
122:       const step = this.observer.step({maxSubjects:25,maxEvents:200,maxDurationMs:2});
123:       if (step.passComplete) break;
124:       await new Promise<void>(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
125:     }
126:     if (this.stopped) return;
127:     const loops = this.store.db.prepare(
128:       "SELECT scope_id,subject_id,payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE kind='loop' AND admitted=1"
129:     ).all() as { scope_id: string; subject_id: string; payload_json: string }[];
130:     await Promise.allSettled(loops.map(async (row) => {
131:       let path: unknown;
132:       try { path = (JSON.parse(row.payload_json) as Record<string, unknown>).state_path; } catch { /* below */ }
133:       if (typeof path !== "string" || !path || path.length > 4096) {
134:         if (!this.stopped) this.store.write(this.observer.owner, () => {
135:           // Do not replace newer metadata or an explicit owner release.
136:           this.store.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_subjects SET sampled_at=?,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=? AND payload_json=?")
137:             .run(Date.now(), JSON.stringify({ state: "unknown", reason: "invalid_payload", process_liveness: "unknown" }), row.scope_id, row.subject_id, row.payload_json);
138:         });
139:         return;
140:       }
141:       try {
142:         await this.handle("attention.loop", {
143:           scope_id: row.scope_id, loop_id: row.subject_id,
144:           state_path: path,
145:         });
146:       } catch { /* source errors never suppress other readers */ }
147:     }));
148:     if (this.stopped) return;
149:     for (const scope of this.store.listScopes()) {
150:       try { this.experience.enqueue(scope.scope_id); }
151:       catch { /* Keep observed items; one unavailable scope must not block other notices. */ }
152:     }
153:     if (this.sender && !this.delivering) {
154:       // Only this service owns the local idempotent sender. A failed receipt
155:       // write leaves `sending`; reconcile it before the next bounded batch.
156:       if (this.deliveryRecoveryNeeded) {
157:         this.experience.recoverDeliveries();
158:         this.deliveryRecoveryNeeded = false;
159:       }
160:       this.delivering = true;
161:       this.deliveryDone = Promise.resolve().then(() => this.experience.drainBatch(this.sender!, { signal: this.deliveryAbort.signal }))
162:         .then(() => {})
163:         .catch(() => { this.deliveryRecoveryNeeded = true; })
164:         .finally(() => { this.delivering = false; });
165:     }
166:   }
167:   stop(): Promise<void> {
168:     if (this.stopDone) return this.stopDone;
169:     this.stopped = true;
170:     this.deliveryAbort.abort();
171:     this.epoch++;
172:     this.dirty = false;
173:     if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
174:     this.timer = undefined;
175:     for (const slot of this.readers.values()) slot.controller.abort();
176:     this.stopDone = Promise.allSettled([this.scanDone, this.deliveryDone, ...this.logicalReads]).then(() => {});
177:     return this.stopDone;
178:   }
179:   private loopBinding(scope: string, id: string): LoopBinding | undefined {
180:     return this.store.db.prepare(
181:       "SELECT binding_hash,admitted,generation,payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=?"
182:     ).get(scope, id) as LoopBinding | undefined;
183:   }
184:   private readLoop(scopeId: string, id: string, path: string): Promise<LoopFacts> {
185:     this.assertRunning();
186:     const epoch = this.epoch;
187:     const binding = attentionDigest([path, id]);
188:     const key = JSON.stringify([scopeId, "loop", id, binding]);
189:     const scope = this.store.getScope(scopeId)!;
190:     // Register the binding before IO, so release and competing RPCs see it.
191:     this.store.write(this.observer.owner, () => {
192:       this.assertRunning();
193:       const old = this.loopBinding(scopeId, id);
194:       if (old && old.binding_hash !== binding) throw new Error("loop_binding_conflict");
195:       if (old) {
196:         const payload = loopPayload(old.payload_json);
197:         if (!payload || payload.reason === "owner_released") return;
198:         const count = (this.store.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_subjects WHERE admitted=1").get() as { n: number }).n;
199:         if (old.generation !== this.observer.owner.generation || (!old.admitted && count < 100))
200:           this.store.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_subjects SET admitted=?,generation=?,sampled_at=0,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=?")
201:             .run(old.admitted || count < 100 ? 1 : 0, this.observer.owner.generation,
202:               JSON.stringify({ ...payload, state: "unknown", reason: "read_pending" }), scopeId, id);
203:         return;
204:       }
205:       const count = (this.store.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_subjects WHERE admitted=1").get() as { n: number }).n;
206:       this.store.db.prepare(
207:         "INSERT INTO attention_subjects(scope_id,kind,subject_id,binding_hash,source_version,admitted,discovered_at,sampled_at,generation,payload_json) VALUES(?,'loop',?,?,0,?,?,?,?,?)"
208:       ).run(scopeId, id, binding, count < 100 ? 1 : 0, Date.now(), 0, this.observer.owner.generation,
209:         JSON.stringify({ state_path: path, state: "unknown", reason: count < 100 ? "read_pending" : "capacity_exceeded" }));
210:     });
211:     const old = this.loopBinding(scopeId, id)!;
212:     const failure = (reason: ReadFailure): LoopFacts => ({ loop_id: id, path, state: "unknown",
213:       process_liveness: "unknown", coverage: "uncovered", gap: "snapshot_unavailable", reason });
214:     const payload = loopPayload(old.payload_json);
215:     if (!payload) return Promise.resolve(failure("invalid_payload"));
216:     if (payload.reason === "owner_released")
217:       return Promise.resolve({ state: "unknown", reason: "owner_released" });
218:     const existing = this.readers.get(key);
219:     if (existing) return existing.logical;
220:     const commit = (facts: LoopFacts): LoopFacts => {
221:       // No await from the lifecycle check through the owner-fenced commit.
222:       if (this.stopped || epoch !== this.epoch) return facts;
223:       this.store.write(this.observer.owner, () => {
224:         const current = this.loopBinding(scopeId, id);
225:         const currentPayload = current ? loopPayload(current.payload_json) : null;
226:         if (!current || current.binding_hash !== binding || current.generation !== this.observer.owner.generation
227:           || current.admitted !== old.admitted || !currentPayload || currentPayload.reason === "owner_released") return;
228:         this.store.db.prepare(
229:           "UPDATE attention_subjects SET sampled_at=?,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=?"
230:         ).run(Date.now(), JSON.stringify({ ...facts, state_path: path,
231:           reason: facts.reason ?? (!current.admitted ? "capacity_exceeded" : facts.state === "source_missing"
232:             ? "source_missing" : facts.recovery_required ? "recovery_required" : "snapshot_only") }), scopeId, id);
233:       });
234:       return facts;
235:     };
236:     if (!old.admitted || this.readers.size >= 100)
237:       return Promise.resolve(commit(failure("read_capacity_exceeded")));
238:     const controller = new AbortController();
239:     let reason: ReadFailure = "aborted";
240:     let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
241:     let abortListener: (() => void) | undefined;
242:     const aborted = new Promise<LoopFacts>((resolve) => {
243:       abortListener = () => resolve(failure(reason));
244:       controller.signal.addEventListener("abort", abortListener, { once: true });
245:       timer = setTimeout(() => { reason = "timeout"; controller.abort(); }, this.readTimeoutMs);
246:     });
247:     const physical = Promise.resolve().then(async () => {
248:       controller.signal.throwIfAborted();
249:       const reader = this.options.loopReader ?? (await import("./attention-loop-source.js")).readAttentionLoop;
250:       controller.signal.throwIfAborted();
251:       return await reader({ repo: scope.repo_path, path, loop_id: id, signal: controller.signal });
252:     }).then((value) => validLoopFacts(value, id) ? value : failure("invalid_payload"), (error: unknown) =>
253:       failure(controller.signal.aborted ? reason : error instanceof SyntaxError
254:         || (error instanceof Error && error.name === "ZodError") ? "invalid_payload" : "read_failed"));
255:     // Physical settlement has no DB access, even if the logical deadline passed.
256:     const settled = physical.finally(() => { this.readers.delete(key); });
257:     const logical = Promise.race([settled, aborted]).then(commit).finally(() => {
258:       if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
259:       if (abortListener) controller.signal.removeEventListener("abort", abortListener);
260:       this.logicalReads.delete(logical);
261:     });
262:     this.readers.set(key, { controller, logical });
263:     this.logicalReads.add(logical);
264:     return logical;
265:   }
266:   async handle(method: string, params: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
267:     this.assertRunning();
268:     if (!params || typeof params !== "object")
269:       throw new Error("explicit_scope_required");
270:     const p = params as Record<string, unknown>;
271:     const str = (key: string) => {
272:       const value = p[key];
273:       if (typeof value !== "string" || !value || value.length > 4096)
274:         throw new Error(`invalid_${key}`);
275:       return value;
276:     };
277:     if (method === "attention.watch") {
278:       if (p["default_tenant"] !== true)
279:         throw new Error("explicit_default_tenant_required");
280:       const scope = this.store.registerScope(
281:         this.observer.owner,
282:         str("task_id"),
283:         str("repo_path")
284:       );
285:       await this.requestScan("watch");
286:       this.assertRunning();
287:       return this.observer.inventory(scope.scope_id);
288:     }
289:     const scopeId = str("scope_id");
290:     if (!this.store.getScope(scopeId)) throw new Error("not_found");
291:     if (method === "attention.recovery") {
292:       if (Array.isArray(p) || Object.keys(p).some(key => !["scope_id","after","limit"].includes(key)) || (p["after"] !== undefined && typeof p["after"] !== "string") || (p["limit"] !== undefined && typeof p["limit"] !== "number")) throw new Error("invalid_recovery_request");
293:       return readAttentionRecovery(this.store,this.observer.owner,{scope_id:scopeId,...(p["after"] === undefined ? {} : {after:p["after"] as string}),...(p["limit"] === undefined ? {} : {limit:p["limit"] as number})});
294:     }
295:     if (method === "attention.release") {
296:       this.store.release(
297:         this.observer.owner,
298:         scopeId,
299:         str("kind"),
300:         str("subject_id"),
301:         Number(p["expected_version"])
302:       );
303:       return {
304:         released: true,
305:         execution_affected: false,
306:         coverage: "uncovered",
307:       };
308:     }
309:     if (method === "attention.loop")
310:       return await this.readLoop(scopeId, str("loop_id"), str("state_path"));
311:     if (method === "attention.ci") {
312:       const { AttentionCiReader } = await import("@osstack/github");
313:       this.assertRunning();

316:         repo: str("github_repo"),
317:         pr: p["pr"],
318:         head: str("head"),
319:         required: p["required"],
320:       });
321:     }
322:     if (method === "attention.brief")
323:       return this.experience.brief(scopeId, str("actor"));
324:     if (method === "attention.posture") {
325:       const posture = str("posture");
326:       if (!["quiet", "away", "attended"].includes(posture))
327:         throw new Error("invalid_posture");
328:       this.experience.posture(
329:         scopeId,
330:         str("actor"),
331:         posture as "quiet" | "away" | "attended"
332:       );
333:       return { posture };
334:     }
335:     if (method === "attention.seen") {
336:       if (!Array.isArray(p["revisions"])) throw new Error("invalid_revisions");
337:       this.experience.seen(scopeId, str("actor"), p["revisions"]);
338:       return { marked: true };
339:     }
340:     if (method === "attention.foreground") {
341:       if (!Array.isArray(p["subjects"])) throw new Error("invalid_subjects");
342:       return this.experience.foreground(scopeId, p["subjects"]);
343:     }
344:     if (method === "attention.handoff")
345:       return this.experience.handoff(scopeId, str("client_id"), str("token"));
346:     if (method === "attention.turn-end")
347:       return this.experience.turnEnd(
348:         scopeId,
349:         str("client_id"),
350:         str("token"),
351:         str("role")
352:       );
353:     if (method === "attention.deliveries")
354:       return this.store.db
355:         .prepare(
356:           "SELECT * FROM attention_deliveries WHERE scope_id=? AND delivery_id>? ORDER BY delivery_id LIMIT 100"
357:         )
358:         .all(scopeId, typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : "");
359:     if (method === "attention.inventory")
360:       return this.observer.inventory(
361:         scopeId,
362:         Date.now(),
363:         typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : ""
364:       );
365:     if (method === "attention.inbox") {

368:         typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : ""

379:         items,
380:         counts,
381:         next_after: items.length === 100 ? items.at(-1)!.item_id : null,
382:         coverage: this.observer.inventory(scopeId).coverage,
383:       });
384:     }
385:     if (method === "attention.get") {
386:       const item = this.store.getItem(scopeId, str("item_id"));
387:       if (!item) throw new Error("not_found");
388:       return item;
389:     }
390:     throw new Error("unsupported_attention_method");
391:   }
392: }

237:   /** Kernel locks may be released; the persistent operation fence survives every failure. */

239:   complete():Promise<void>{return this.track(()=>this.doComplete());}
240:   private async doComplete(): Promise<void> {
241:     this.assertPins();

248:     // A crash at either boundary is safe and the same authorized cleanup is resumable.
249:     await unlink(operation).catch(e=>{if(e.code!=='ENOENT')throw e;});await syncDirectory(this.config.root);

13: /** Common human reservation, serialized with collaborator writes in the same DB. */
14: export function reserveSessionInstruction(
15:   db: Database.Database,
16:   path: string,
17:   message: SessionMessageInput,
18:   version: number,
19:   now: number,
20:   takeover = false,
21:   takeoverGrantId?: string
22: ) {
23:   return withWrite(db, path, () => {
24:     const sessions = new SessionRepository(db, path);
25:     if (sessions.getMessage(message.message_id))
26:       return sessions.applyInbound(message, version, now);
27:     if ((db.prepare("SELECT p.message_id FROM attention_dispatch_phases p JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=p.message_id WHERE m.session_id=? AND p.phase='cancelled_before_dispatch'").all(message.session_id) as Array<{ message_id: string }>).some(row => !isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db, row.message_id))) throw new Error("dispatch_inconsistent");
28:     if ((db.prepare("SELECT message_id FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND direction='inbound' AND state IN ('accepted','failed') AND message_id NOT IN (SELECT message_id FROM attention_receipt_resolutions)").all(message.session_id) as Array<{ message_id: string }>).some(row => !isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db, row.message_id)))
29:       throw new Error("delivery_uncertain");
30:     if (db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_dispatch_phases p JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=p.message_id WHERE m.session_id=? AND p.phase='cancelled_before_dispatch' AND m.state='applied'").get(message.session_id)) throw new Error("dispatch_inconsistent");
31:     if (
32:       db
33:         .prepare(
34:           "SELECT 1 FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON a.grant_id=g.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=? AND a.status IN ('checking','reserved') LIMIT 1"
35:         )
36:         .get(message.session_id)
37:     )
38:       throw new Error("continuation_in_flight");
39:     const grant = db
40:       .prepare(
41:         "SELECT grant_id FROM session_continuation_grants WHERE session_id=? AND status='active'"
42:       )
43:       .get(message.session_id) as { grant_id: string } | undefined;
44:     if (grant && takeover && takeoverGrantId !== grant.grant_id)
45:       throw new Error("takeover_grant_changed");
46:     if (grant && !takeover) throw new Error("grant_owned");
47:     const snapshot = sessions.latestSnapshot(message.session_id);
48:     if (snapshot.state === "awaiting_decision" && snapshot.decision === null)
49:       throw new Error("invalid_decision");
50:     const decision = SessionDecision.safeParse(snapshot.decision);
51:     const raw = snapshot.decision as Record<string, unknown> | null;
52:     const legacy =
53:       raw !== null &&
54:       typeof raw === "object" &&
55:       !Array.isArray(raw) &&
56:       Object.keys(raw).every((k) => ["question", "choices"].includes(k)) &&
57:       Array.isArray(raw["choices"]) &&
58:       raw["choices"].every((v) => typeof v === "string") &&
59:       (raw["question"] === undefined || typeof raw["question"] === "string");
60:     if (snapshot.decision !== null && !decision.success && !legacy)
61:       throw new Error("invalid_decision");
62: 
63:     if (
64:       decision.success &&
65:       ["risk_approval", "task_authorization"].includes(
66:         decision.data.continuation?.gate_kind ?? ""
67:       )
68:     )
69:       throw new Error("signed_approval_required");
70:     if (grant)
71:       db.prepare(
72:         "UPDATE session_continuation_grants SET status='blocked',reason='human_takeover',updated_at=? WHERE grant_id=?"
73:       ).run(now, grant.grant_id);
74:     return sessions.applyInbound(message, version, now);
75:   });

77: export interface AttentionAnswer {
78:   scope_id: string;
79:   item_id: string;
80:   action_id: string;
81:   revision: number;
82:   content_hash: string;
83:   text: string;
84:   takeover: boolean;
85:   takeover_grant_id?: string;
86: }
87: interface Action {
88:   action_id: string;
89:   scope_id: string;
90:   item_id: string;
91:   payload_hash: string;
92:   message_id: string;
93:   state: string;
94:   created_at: number;
95:   updated_at: number;
96: }
97: export class AttentionActions {
98:   constructor(readonly store: AttentionStore, readonly owner: AttentionOwner) {}
99:   reserve(input: AttentionAnswer, now = Date.now()) {
100:     if (
101:       !input.action_id ||
102:       input.action_id.length > 256 ||
103:       !input.text.trim() ||
104:       input.text.length > 16000
105:     )
106:       throw new Error("invalid_answer");
107:     return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
108:       const db = this.store.db;
109:       const digest = attentionDigest(input);
110:       const prior = db
111:         .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?")
112:         .get(input.action_id) as Action | undefined;
113:       if (prior) {
114:         if (prior.payload_hash !== digest)
115:           throw new Error("action_id_conflict");
116:         return {
117:           ...this.reconcile(prior, now),
118:           replay: true,
119:           reservation_version: 0,
120:         };
121:       }
122:       const item = this.store.getItem(input.scope_id, input.item_id);
123:       const scope = this.store.getScope(input.scope_id);
124:       if (!item || !scope) throw new Error("not_found");
125:       if (
126:         item.state !== "open" ||
127:         item.revision !== input.revision ||
128:         item.content_hash !== input.content_hash
129:       )
130:         throw new Error("stale_item");
131:       if (
132:         db
133:           .prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_actions WHERE item_id=?")
134:           .get(item.item_id)
135:       )
136:         throw new Error("action_already_reserved");
137:       const repo = new SessionRepository(db, this.store.path);
138:       const source = repo.getSession(item.session_id);
139:       const latest = repo.latestSnapshot(item.session_id);
140:       if (source && source.state !== "awaiting_decision")
141:         throw new Error("session_not_awaiting_decision");
142:       if (
143:         !source ||
144:         source.task_id !== scope.task_id ||
145:         realpathSync(source.repo_path) !== scope.repo_path ||
146:         latest.turn_id !== item.turn_id ||
147:         source.version !== item.source_version ||
148:         attentionDigest(SessionDecision.parse(latest.decision)) !==
149:           item.content_hash
150:       )
151:         throw new Error("source_changed");
152:       if (
153:         !db
154:           .prepare(
155:             "SELECT 1 FROM tasks t JOIN runs r ON r.task_id=t.task_id WHERE t.task_id=? AND r.run_id=? AND t.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.tenant_id IS NULL"
156:           )
157:           .get(scope.task_id, source.run_id)
158:       )
159:         throw new Error("source_changed");
160:       const messageId = `attention-${randomUUID()}`;
161:       const reservation = reserveSessionInstruction(
162:         db,
163:         this.store.path,
164:         {
165:           message_id: messageId,
166:           session_id: item.session_id,
167:           turn_id: item.turn_id,
168:           direction: "inbound",
169:           kind: "instruction.send",
170:           payload: { text: input.text },
171:           state: "accepted",
172:           created_at: now / 1000,
173:         },
174:         source.version,
175:         now / 1000,
176:         input.takeover,
177:         input.takeover_grant_id
178:       );
179:       db.prepare(
180:         "INSERT INTO attention_actions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,'reserved',?,?)"
181:       ).run(
182:         input.action_id,
183:         input.scope_id,
184:         input.item_id,
185:         digest,
186:         messageId,
187:         now,
188:         now
189:       );
190:       db.prepare(
191:         "UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_reserved',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id=?"
192:       ).run(now, item.item_id);
193:       return {
194:         action_id: input.action_id,
195:         scope_id: input.scope_id,
196:         item_id: input.item_id,
197:         payload_hash: digest,
198:         message_id: messageId,
199:         state: "reserved",
200:         created_at: now,
201:         updated_at: now,
202:         replay: false,
203:         reservation_version: reservation.session_version,
204:       };
205:     });
206:   }
207:   replay(input: AttentionAnswer, applyingMessageId?: string) {
208:     const prior = this.store.db
209:       .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?")
210:       .get(input.action_id) as Action | undefined;
211:     if (!prior) return undefined;
212:     if (prior.payload_hash !== attentionDigest(input))
213:       throw new Error("action_id_conflict");
214:     if (getEngineeringDispatchPhase(this.store.db, prior.message_id)?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") return { ...this.reconcile(prior), replay: true };
215:     if (prior.message_id === applyingMessageId)
216:       return { ...prior, state: "in_flight", replay: true };
217:     return { ...this.reconcile(prior), replay: true };
218:   }
219:   abandon(
220:     input: {
221:       scope_id: string;
222:       session_id: string;
223:       message_id: string;
224:       expected_version: number;
225:       reason: string;
226:       evidence_sha256: string;
227:     },
228:     now = Date.now()
229:   ) {
230:     if (
231:       !input.reason ||
232:       input.reason.length > 1000 ||
233:       !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(input.evidence_sha256)
234:     )
235:       throw new Error("resolution_evidence_required");
236:     return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
237:       const db = this.store.db;
238:       const session = new SessionRepository(db, this.store.path).getSession(
239:         input.session_id
240:       );
241:       if (!session || session.version !== input.expected_version)
242:         throw new Error("version_conflict");
243:       const scope = this.store.getScope(input.scope_id);
244:       if (
245:         !scope ||
246:         scope.task_id !== session.task_id ||
247:         scope.repo_path !== realpathSync(session.repo_path)
248:       )
249:         throw new Error("scope_mismatch");
250:       const message = db
251:         .prepare(
252:           "SELECT state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id=? AND session_id=? AND direction='inbound'"
253:         )
254:         .get(input.message_id, input.session_id) as
255:         | { state: string }
256:         | undefined;
257:       const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(db, input.message_id);
258:       if (phase?.phase === "reserved") throw new Error("not_dispatched_use_cancel");
259:       if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") {
260:         if (!isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db, input.message_id)) throw new Error("dispatch_inconsistent");
261:         return { disposition: "cancelled_before_dispatch", message_id: input.message_id, replayed: true, execution_outcome: "not_dispatched", next_action: "The original answer is cancelled. It cannot be sent again." };
262:       }
263:       if (!message || !["accepted", "failed"].includes(message.state))
264:         throw new Error("receipt_not_uncertain");
265:       if (
266:         db
267:           .prepare(
268:             "SELECT 1 FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON a.grant_id=g.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=? AND a.status IN ('checking','reserved')"
269:           )
270:           .get(input.session_id)
271:       )
272:         throw new Error("continuation_in_flight");
273:       const prior = db
274:         .prepare(
275:           "SELECT * FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?"
276:         )
277:         .get(input.message_id) as
278:         | { reason: string; evidence_sha256: string }
279:         | undefined;
280:       if (
281:         prior &&
282:         (prior.reason !== input.reason ||
283:           prior.evidence_sha256 !== input.evidence_sha256)
284:       )
285:         throw new Error("resolution_conflict");
286:       db.prepare(
287:         "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO attention_receipt_resolutions VALUES(?,?,?,'abandoned_unknown',?,?,?)"
288:       ).run(
289:         input.message_id,
290:         input.session_id,
291:         input.expected_version,
292:         input.reason,
293:         input.evidence_sha256,
294:         now
295:       );
296:       db.prepare(
297:         "UPDATE attention_actions SET state='abandoned_unknown',updated_at=? WHERE message_id=?"
298:       ).run(now, input.message_id);
299:       db.prepare(
300:         "UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_abandoned',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM attention_actions WHERE message_id=?) AND state<>'answer_abandoned'"
301:       ).run(now, input.message_id);
302:       return {
303:         disposition: "abandoned_unknown",
304:         message_id: input.message_id,
305:         replayed: false,
306:         execution_outcome: "unknown",
307:         next_action:
308:           "New manual instruction only; original message is never resent",
309:       };
310:     });
311:   }
312:   recover(): void {
313:     const rows = this.store.db
314:       .prepare(
315:         "SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE state IN ('reserved','uncertain')"
316:       )
317:       .all() as Action[];
318:     for (const row of rows) this.reconcile(row);
319:   }
320:   reconcile(action: Action, now = Date.now()): Action {
321:     return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
322:       const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(this.store.db, action.message_id);
323:       if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch") {
324:         if (!isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.store.db, action.message_id)) return { ...action, state: "needs_reconciliation" };
325:         return { ...action, state: "cancelled_before_dispatch" };
326:       }
327:       if (
328:         this.store.db
329:           .prepare(
330:             "SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?"
331:           )
332:           .get(action.message_id)
333:       )
334:         return { ...action, state: "abandoned_unknown" };
335:       const receipt = this.store.db
336:         .prepare("SELECT state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id=?")
337:         .get(action.message_id) as { state: string } | undefined;
338:       if (phase?.phase === "reserved") {
339:         return { ...action, state: action.state === "reserved" && receipt?.state === "accepted" ? "reserved" : "needs_reconciliation" };
340:       }
341:       const state =
342:         receipt?.state === "applied"
343:           ? "applied"
344:           : receipt?.state === "failed"
345:           ? "uncertain"
346:           : "uncertain";
347:       this.store.db
348:         .prepare(
349:           "UPDATE attention_actions SET state=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND state<>?"
350:         )
351:         .run(state, now, action.action_id, state);
352:       this.store.db
353:         .prepare(
354:           "UPDATE attention_items SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id=? AND state<>?"
355:         )
356:         .run(
357:           state === "applied" ? "answer_applied" : "answer_uncertain",
358:           now,
359:           action.item_id,
360:           state === "applied" ? "answer_applied" : "answer_uncertain"
361:         );
362:       return { ...action, state, updated_at: state === action.state ? action.updated_at : now };
363:     });
364:   }
365: }

1: import { createHash, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
2: import { realpathSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
3: import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
4: import { SessionDecision } from "@osstack/protocol";
5: import { withWrite } from "./db.js";
6: 
7: export function attentionDigest(value: unknown): string {
8:   const canonical = (v: unknown): string => {
9:     if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
10:     if (v !== null && typeof v === "object")
11:       return `{${Object.entries(v)
12:         .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0))
13:         .map(([k, x]) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(x)}`)
14:         .join(",")}}`;
15:     return JSON.stringify(v);
16:   };
17:   return createHash("sha256").update(canonical(value)).digest("hex");
18: }

20:   instance_id: string;
21:   generation: number;

58: export class AttentionStore {
59:   constructor(readonly db: Database.Database, readonly path: string) {}
60:   /** Only called AFTER the daemon's process lock is acquired. Not an executor lease. */
61:   claimOwner(instanceId: string): AttentionOwner {
62:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, () => {
63:       this.db
64:         .prepare(
65:           `INSERT INTO daemon_owner(singleton,home_id,instance_id,generation) VALUES(1,?,?,1)
66:         ON CONFLICT(singleton) DO UPDATE SET instance_id=excluded.instance_id,generation=generation+1`
67:         )
68:         .run(randomUUID(), instanceId);
69:       return this.db
70:         .prepare(
71:           "SELECT home_id,instance_id,generation FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1"
72:         )
73:         .get() as AttentionOwner;
74:     });
75:   }
76:   assertOwner(owner: AttentionOwner): void {
77:     if (
78:       !this.db
79:         .prepare(
80:           "SELECT 1 FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1 AND home_id=? AND instance_id=? AND generation=?"
81:         )
82:         .get(owner.home_id, owner.instance_id, owner.generation)
83:     )
84:       throw new Error("stale_owner");
85:   }
86:   write<T>(owner: AttentionOwner, fn: () => T): T {
87:     if (this.db.inTransaction) {
88:       this.assertOwner(owner);
89:       return fn();
90:     }
91:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, () => {
92:       this.assertOwner(owner);
93:       return fn();
94:     });
95:   }
96:   registerScope(
97:     owner: AttentionOwner,
98:     taskId: string,
99:     repoPath: string
100:   ): AttentionScope {
101:     const canonicalRepo = realpathSync(repoPath);
102:     if (!statSync(canonicalRepo).isDirectory())

107:           "SELECT created_at FROM tasks WHERE task_id=? AND tenant_id IS NULL"
108:         )

110:       if (!task) throw new Error("not_found");
111:       if (
112:         (
113:           this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_scopes").get() as {
114:             n: number;
115:           }
116:         ).n >= 100 &&
117:         !this.db
118:           .prepare(
119:             "SELECT 1 FROM attention_scopes WHERE task_id=? AND repo_path=?"
120:           )
121:           .get(taskId, canonicalRepo)
122:       )
123:         throw new Error("scope_capacity");
124:       const scopeId = attentionDigest([
125:         owner.home_id,
126:         taskId,
127:         canonicalRepo,
128:         task.created_at,
129:       ]);
130:       this.db
131:         .prepare(
132:           `INSERT INTO attention_scopes(scope_id,task_id,repo_path,generation) VALUES(?,?,?,?)
133:         ON CONFLICT(scope_id) DO NOTHING`
134:         )
135:         .run(scopeId, taskId, canonicalRepo, owner.generation);
136:       return this.getScope(scopeId)!;
137:     });
138:   }
139:   getScope(scopeId: string): AttentionScope | undefined {
140:     return this.db

143:   }
144:   listScopes(): AttentionScope[] {
