147:       .all() as AttentionScope[];
148:   }
149:   getItem(scopeId: string, itemId: string): AttentionItem | undefined {
150:     const row = this.db
151:       .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND item_id=?")
152:       .get(scopeId, itemId) as ItemRow | undefined;
153:     return row ? parseItem(row) : undefined;
154:   }
155:   listItems(scopeId: string, after = "", limit = 100): AttentionItem[] {
156:     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100)
157:       throw new Error("invalid_limit");
158:     return (
159:       this.db
160:         .prepare(
161:           "SELECT * FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND item_id>? ORDER BY item_id LIMIT ?"
162:         )
163:         .all(scopeId, after, limit) as ItemRow[]
164:     ).map(parseItem);
165:   }
166:   projectDecision(
167:     owner: AttentionOwner,
168:     input: {
169:       scopeId: string;
170:       sessionId: string;
171:       turnId: string;
172:       sourceVersion: number;
173:       decision: unknown;
174:       now: number;
175:     }
176:   ): AttentionItem {
177:     const decision = SessionDecision.parse(input.decision);
178:     const contentHash = attentionDigest(decision);
179:     const itemId = attentionDigest([
180:       input.scopeId,
181:       input.sessionId,
182:       input.turnId,
183:       decision.decision_id,
184:     ]);
185:     if (
186:       !Number.isSafeInteger(input.sourceVersion) ||
187:       input.sourceVersion < 1 ||
188:       !Number.isFinite(input.now)
189:     )
190:       throw new Error("invalid_source_version");
191:     return this.write(owner, () => {
192:       if (!this.getScope(input.scopeId)) throw new Error("not_found");
193:       const existing = this.getItem(input.scopeId, itemId);
194:       if (existing && existing.content_hash !== contentHash)
195:         throw new Error("decision_content_conflict");
196:       if (existing && existing.source_version > input.sourceVersion)
197:         throw new Error("stale_source");
198:       this.db
199:         .prepare(
200:           `INSERT INTO attention_items(item_id,scope_id,session_id,turn_id,decision_id,content_hash,decision_json,source_version,created_at,updated_at)
201:         VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(item_id) DO UPDATE SET
202:         revision=revision+CASE WHEN source_version<>excluded.source_version OR state='source_missing' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
203:         state=CASE WHEN state='source_missing' THEN 'open' ELSE state END,
204:         source_version=excluded.source_version,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`
205:         )
206:         .run(
207:           itemId,
208:           input.scopeId,
209:           input.sessionId,
210:           input.turnId,
211:           decision.decision_id,
212:           contentHash,
213:           JSON.stringify(decision),
214:           input.sourceVersion,
215:           input.now,
216:           input.now
217:         );
218:       return this.getItem(input.scopeId, itemId)!;
219:     });
220:   }
221:   release(
222:     owner: AttentionOwner,
223:     scopeId: string,
224:     kind: string,
225:     subjectId: string,
226:     expectedVersion: number
227:   ): void {
228:     if (!["session", "run", "loop"].includes(kind))
229:       throw new Error("invalid_subject_kind");
230:     this.write(owner, () => {
231:       const row = this.db
232:         .prepare(
233:           "SELECT payload_json,source_version FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
234:         )
235:         .get(scopeId, kind, subjectId) as
236:         | { payload_json: string; source_version: number }
237:         | undefined;
238:       if (!row || row.source_version !== expectedVersion)
239:         throw new Error("subject_version_conflict");
240:       this.db
241:         .prepare(
242:           "UPDATE attention_subjects SET admitted=0,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
243:         )
244:         .run(
245:           JSON.stringify({
246:             ...JSON.parse(row.payload_json),
247:             reason: "owner_released",
248:           }),
249:           scopeId,
250:           kind,
251:           subjectId
252:         );
253:     });
254:   }
255:   markMissing(owner: AttentionOwner, scopeId: string, sessionId: string): void {
256:     this.write(owner, () =>
257:       this.db
258:         .prepare(
259:           `UPDATE attention_items SET state='source_missing',revision=revision+1
260:       WHERE scope_id=? AND session_id=? AND state='open'`
261:         )
262:         .run(scopeId, sessionId)
263:     );
264:   }

===== FILE packages/core/src/state/db.ts sha256=bda761c2999f90292d546b6b871ad83567b851cc605767d6d6cc12417f183fee =====
1: // SQLite connection management.
2: //
3: // Decisions locked in plan v0.6:
4: //   - better-sqlite3 (synchronous, single connection per daemon process)
5: //   - WAL mode for concurrent reader/writer safety
6: //   - synchronous=NORMAL (WAL-safe, faster than FULL)
7: //   - 64 MiB page cache
8: //   - foreign_keys ON (so ON DELETE CASCADE works for runs/events on task delete)
9: //
10: // CRITICAL GAP G1 (plan v0.6 §8.3): disk-full must never cause a silent task
11: // write loss. Every write goes through preflightDiskSpace(), which throws
12: // DiskFullError before any INSERT/UPDATE can corrupt or stall a transaction.
13: 
14: import Database from "better-sqlite3";
15: import { statfsSync } from "node:fs";
16: import { dirname } from "node:path";
17: import { CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION, MIGRATIONS } from "./migrations.js";
18: 
19: /** Default safety threshold for disk-full preflight (100 MiB). */
20: export const DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024;
21: 
22: /** Cache size in -kibibyte units. negative = KiB (per SQLite docs). 64 MiB. */
23: const PAGE_CACHE_KIB = -65536;
24: 
25: export class DiskFullError extends Error {
26:   readonly path: string;
27:   readonly freeBytes: number;
28:   readonly minFreeBytes: number;
29:   constructor(path: string, freeBytes: number, minFreeBytes: number) {
30:     super(
31:       `Disk full preflight failed for ${path}: ${freeBytes} bytes free, ` +
32:       `need ≥${minFreeBytes}. Refusing write so Hermes can be notified.`,
33:     );
34:     this.name = "DiskFullError";
35:     this.path = path;
36:     this.freeBytes = freeBytes;
37:     this.minFreeBytes = minFreeBytes;
38:   }
39: }
40: 
41: export interface OpenDbOptions {
42:   /** Absolute path to the SQLite file (parent dir must exist). */
43:   path: string;
44:   /** When false, opens read-only. */
45:   readwrite?: boolean;
46:   /** Override the disk-full safety threshold (bytes). */
47:   minFreeBytes?: number;
48: }
49: 
50: /**
51:  * Run a free-space check against the filesystem holding `path`. Throws
52:  * DiskFullError if below threshold; otherwise returns free byte count.
53:  * Skips the check (returns Infinity) if statfsSync is unsupported on the
54:  * platform — better to allow writes than to crash a daemon on a kernel quirk.
55:  */
56: export function preflightDiskSpace(
57:   path: string,
58:   minFreeBytes: number = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES,
59: ): number {
60:   let stats: { bsize: number; bavail: number };
61:   try {
62:     stats = statfsSync(dirname(path));
63:   } catch {
64:     return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
65:   }
66:   const free = Number(stats.bsize) * Number(stats.bavail);
67:   if (free < minFreeBytes) throw new DiskFullError(path, free, minFreeBytes);
68:   return free;
69: }
70: 
71: /**
72:  * Open the osstack state database, apply pragmas, and run pending migrations.
73:  * Caller owns the lifetime — pair every openDb() with a db.close() in shutdown.
74:  */
75: export function openDb(opts: OpenDbOptions): Database.Database {
76:   const { path, readwrite = true, minFreeBytes = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES } = opts;
77:   if (readwrite) preflightDiskSpace(path, minFreeBytes);
78: 
79:   const db = new Database(path, { readonly: !readwrite });
80: 
81:   try {
82:     // Refuse an unsupported schema before changing journal mode or running DDL,
83:     // including read-only inspection. A failed open never escapes with a live handle.
84:     assertSupportedVersion(db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number);
85:     assertMigrationLineage(db);
86:     // Pragmas are applied per-connection in SQLite. Apply on every open.
87:     if (readwrite) db.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
88:     db.pragma("synchronous = NORMAL");
89:     db.pragma(`cache_size = ${PAGE_CACHE_KIB}`);
90:     db.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");
91:     db.pragma("busy_timeout = 5000");
92: 
93:     if (readwrite) runMigrations(db);
94:     return db;
95:   } catch (error) {
96:     db.close();
97:     throw error;
98:   }
99: }
100: 
101: // This schema is table-additive. Derive expected table introductions once;
102: // a future DROP/RENAME migration must also update this lineage contract.
103: const tableIntroductions = MIGRATIONS.flatMap(migration =>
104:   [...migration.sql.matchAll(/^\s*CREATE\s+TABLE(?:\s+IF\s+NOT\s+EXISTS)?\s+([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\s*\(/gm)]
105:     .map(match => ({ name: match[1]!, version: migration.id })));
106: 
107: /** Reject pure or mixed unreleased Graph schemas before application pragmas or
108:  * DDL. SQLite may itself checkpoint an abandoned WAL on close; logical ledger
109:  * content and user_version must remain intact for the original binary. */
110: function assertMigrationLineage(db: Database.Database): void {
111:   const current = db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number;
112:   const actual = new Set((db.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'").all() as { name: string }[]).map(row => row.name));
113:   // Experimental Graph or Attention tables must never be migrated as released main.
114:   const experimentalTable = (name: string) => name.startsWith("graph_") || name.startsWith("attention_") || name.startsWith("engineering_") || name === "daemon_owner";
115:   if (current < 25 && ![...actual].some(experimentalTable)) return;
116:   const expected = new Set(tableIntroductions.filter(table => table.version <= current).map(table => table.name));
117:   if ([...expected].some(name => !actual.has(name))
118:     || [...actual].some(name => experimentalTable(name) && !expected.has(name))) {
119:     throw new Error("GRAPH_EXPERIMENTAL_SCHEMA_CONFLICT: preserve this home and reconcile with its original build; do not reset user_version or reuse it for the integrated runtime");
120:   }
121: }
122: 
123: function assertSupportedVersion(current: number): void {
124:   if (current > CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
125:     throw new Error(`Migration state inconsistent: user_version=${current}, expected=${CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION}`);
126:   }
127: }
128: 
129: /**
130:  * Apply pending migrations idempotently. `user_version` PRAGMA tracks progress.
131:  * The version pragma guarantees forward-only application. Many migrations
132:  * contain non-idempotent CREATE/ALTER statements; never reset user_version.
133:  */
134: export function runMigrations(db: Database.Database): void {
135:   const currentRow = db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number;
136:   let current = Number(currentRow);
137:   assertSupportedVersion(current);
138:   assertMigrationLineage(db);
139:   for (const m of MIGRATIONS) {
140:     if (m.id <= current) continue;
141:     db.exec("BEGIN");
142:     try {
143:       db.exec(m.sql);
144:       db.pragma(`user_version = ${m.id}`);
145:       db.exec("COMMIT");
146:       current = m.id;
147:     } catch (err) {
148:       db.exec("ROLLBACK");
149:       throw err;
150:     }
151:   }
152:   if (current !== CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
153:     throw new Error(
154:       `Migration state inconsistent: user_version=${current}, expected=${CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION}`,
155:     );
156:   }
157: }
158: 
159: /**
160:  * Run a function inside a transaction with preflight. Use for any write path
161:  * called from the daemon hot loop. Wraps better-sqlite3's transaction() so
162:  * disk-space errors fire BEFORE BEGIN, not mid-transaction.
163:  */
164: export function withWrite<T>(
165:   db: Database.Database,
166:   path: string,
167:   fn: (db: Database.Database) => T,
168:   minFreeBytes: number = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES,
169: ): T {
170:   preflightDiskSpace(path, minFreeBytes);
171:   const tx = db.transaction(fn);
172:   return tx(db);
173: }

===== FILE packages/core/src/state/engineering-ddl.ts sha256=e7067bfc981362f7d9740ac574bebe87c246e62fbd1bb0e2e0ac71b6b18fb5c3 =====
1: /** Additive Session-adoption pilot schema. The existing attention action remains
2:  * the only outbound message authority; this table only gates its first dispatch. */
3: export const ENGINEERING_DDL = `
4: CREATE TABLE engineering_missions (
5:   mission_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, scope_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES attention_scopes(scope_id),
6:   task_id TEXT NOT NULL, run_id TEXT NOT NULL, session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
7:   repo_path TEXT NOT NULL,
8:   source_instance_id TEXT NOT NULL, source_generation INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(source_generation=1),
9:   owner_principal_id TEXT NOT NULL, owner_client_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
10:   goal TEXT NOT NULL, completion_level TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(completion_level='observed_turn'),
11:   state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('observing','awaiting_decision','answer_recorded','needs_reconciliation','completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')),
12:   revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(revision>0), stop_intent_id TEXT,
13:   created_at INTEGER NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL, observed_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
14:   policy_digest TEXT NOT NULL, observation_proof_json TEXT
15: );
16: CREATE UNIQUE INDEX engineering_active_source ON engineering_missions(session_id)
17:   WHERE state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended');
18: CREATE INDEX engineering_mission_scan ON engineering_missions(created_at,mission_id);
19: CREATE TABLE engineering_waits (
20:   wait_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, mission_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES engineering_missions(mission_id),
21:   item_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES attention_items(item_id),
22:   revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(revision>0), decision_ref_json TEXT NOT NULL,
23:   state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('open','deferred','answer_recorded','delivery_unknown','cleared','superseded','needs_reconciliation','cancelled_before_dispatch')),
24:   action_id TEXT UNIQUE REFERENCES attention_actions(action_id), revisit_at INTEGER, reason TEXT,
25:   reminder_sent INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(reminder_sent IN (0,1)),
26:   created_at INTEGER NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
27: );
28: CREATE INDEX engineering_waits_mission ON engineering_waits(mission_id,created_at,wait_id);
29: CREATE TABLE engineering_events (
30:   seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, mission_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES engineering_missions(mission_id),
31:   kind TEXT NOT NULL, actor TEXT, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(payload_json)<=32768), created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
32: );
33: CREATE INDEX engineering_events_mission ON engineering_events(mission_id,seq);
34: CREATE TABLE engineering_requests (
35:   principal_id TEXT NOT NULL, client_session_id TEXT NOT NULL, request_id TEXT NOT NULL,
36:   operation TEXT NOT NULL, payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL, mission_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES engineering_missions(mission_id),
37:   response_json TEXT NOT NULL, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
38:   PRIMARY KEY(principal_id,client_session_id,request_id)
39: );
40: CREATE TABLE engineering_interactions (
41:   token_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY, principal_id TEXT NOT NULL, client_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
42:   mission_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES engineering_missions(mission_id), mission_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
43:   wait_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES engineering_waits(wait_id), item_id TEXT NOT NULL,
44:   item_revision INTEGER NOT NULL, content_hash TEXT NOT NULL, issued_at INTEGER NOT NULL, expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL
45: );
46: CREATE INDEX engineering_request_budget ON engineering_requests(mission_id,operation);
47: CREATE INDEX engineering_interactions_expiry ON engineering_interactions(expires_at);
48: CREATE INDEX engineering_interactions_mission ON engineering_interactions(mission_id,issued_at);
49: CREATE TABLE attention_dispatch_phases (
50:   action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES attention_actions(action_id),
51:   message_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES session_messages(message_id),
52:   mission_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES engineering_missions(mission_id),
53:   principal_id TEXT NOT NULL, client_session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
54:   protocol_version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(protocol_version=1),
55:   phase TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(phase IN ('reserved','dispatch_committed','cancelled_before_dispatch')),
56:   source_instance_id TEXT NOT NULL, source_generation INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(source_generation=1),
57:   original_turn_id TEXT NOT NULL, source_version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(source_version>0),
58:   reservation_version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(reservation_version=source_version+1),
59:   reserved_by_generation INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(reserved_by_generation>0), committed_by_generation INTEGER,
60:   stop_intent_id TEXT, proof_json TEXT,
61:   created_at INTEGER NOT NULL, updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
62:   CHECK((phase='reserved' AND committed_by_generation IS NULL AND stop_intent_id IS NULL AND proof_json IS NULL)
63:      OR (phase='dispatch_committed' AND committed_by_generation>0 AND stop_intent_id IS NULL AND proof_json IS NULL)
64:      OR (phase='cancelled_before_dispatch' AND committed_by_generation IS NULL AND stop_intent_id IS NOT NULL AND proof_json IS NOT NULL))
65: );
66: CREATE INDEX attention_dispatch_scan ON attention_dispatch_phases(phase,created_at,action_id);
67: CREATE INDEX attention_dispatch_mission ON attention_dispatch_phases(mission_id,action_id);
68: CREATE TRIGGER attention_dispatch_no_rewind BEFORE UPDATE ON attention_dispatch_phases
69: WHEN OLD.phase<>'reserved' OR NEW.phase='reserved' OR OLD.action_id<>NEW.action_id OR OLD.message_id<>NEW.message_id
70:   OR OLD.mission_id<>NEW.mission_id OR OLD.source_instance_id<>NEW.source_instance_id
71:   OR OLD.source_generation<>NEW.source_generation OR OLD.original_turn_id<>NEW.original_turn_id
72:   OR OLD.source_version<>NEW.source_version OR OLD.reservation_version<>NEW.reservation_version
73:   OR OLD.principal_id<>NEW.principal_id OR OLD.client_session_id<>NEW.client_session_id
74:   OR OLD.protocol_version<>NEW.protocol_version OR OLD.reserved_by_generation<>NEW.reserved_by_generation
75: BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'dispatch_phase_immutable'); END;
76: CREATE TABLE engineering_completions (
77:   action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES attention_actions(action_id),
78:   message_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES session_messages(message_id),
79:   mission_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES engineering_missions(mission_id),
80:   completed_turn_id TEXT NOT NULL, session_version INTEGER NOT NULL,
81:   proof_json TEXT NOT NULL, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
82: );
83: CREATE INDEX engineering_completion_mission ON engineering_completions(mission_id,completed_turn_id,session_version);
84: `;

===== FILE packages/core/src/state/engineering-dispatch.ts sha256=36b03aea0604875d67c41ac2c2c1e807d0e4cc7d2dc593ac9b4c2a24ae469abd =====
1: import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
2: 
3: export interface EngineeringDispatchPhase {
4:   action_id: string; message_id: string; mission_id: string; principal_id: string; client_session_id: string;
5:   protocol_version: 1; phase: "reserved" | "dispatch_committed" | "cancelled_before_dispatch";
6:   source_instance_id: string; source_generation: number; original_turn_id: string;
7:   source_version: number; reservation_version: number; reserved_by_generation: number;
8:   committed_by_generation: number | null; stop_intent_id: string | null; proof_json: string | null;
9:   created_at: number; updated_at: number;
10: }
11: export function getEngineeringDispatchPhase(db: Database.Database, messageId: string): EngineeringDispatchPhase | undefined {
12:   return db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE message_id=?").get(messageId) as EngineeringDispatchPhase | undefined;
13: }
14: 
15: /** A phase label alone cannot release the uncertain-message guard. The original
16:  * message/action, immutable cancellation transition, and durable intent agree. */
17: export function isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(db: Database.Database, messageId: string): boolean {
18:   const phase = getEngineeringDispatchPhase(db, messageId);
19:   if (!phase || phase.phase !== "cancelled_before_dispatch") return false;
20:   const bound = db.prepare(`SELECT m.state,a.state action_state,a.action_id,a.message_id,i.session_id,i.turn_id,e.kind,e.payload_json
21:     FROM attention_actions a JOIN session_messages m ON m.message_id=a.message_id
22:     JOIN attention_items i ON i.item_id=a.item_id AND i.scope_id=a.scope_id
23:     JOIN engineering_missions x ON x.mission_id=? AND x.session_id=m.session_id AND x.scope_id=a.scope_id
24:     JOIN engineering_events e ON e.mission_id=x.mission_id
25:     WHERE a.action_id=? AND a.message_id=? AND i.session_id=m.session_id AND i.turn_id=m.turn_id AND e.seq=?`).get(
26:       phase.mission_id, phase.action_id, messageId, readProofSequence(phase.proof_json),
27:     ) as { state: string; action_state: string; action_id: string; message_id: string; session_id: string; turn_id: string; kind: string; payload_json: string } | undefined;
28:   if (!bound || bound.state === "applied" || bound.action_state !== "cancelled_before_dispatch" || bound.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || !["stop_requested", "observation_ended"].includes(bound.kind)
29:     || db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(messageId)) return false;
30:   try {
31:     const proof = JSON.parse(phase.proof_json ?? "null") as Record<string, unknown> | null;
32:     const event = JSON.parse(bound.payload_json) as Record<string, unknown>;
33:     return proof?.["version"] === 1 && proof["prior_phase"] === "reserved"
34:       && proof["action_id"] === phase.action_id && proof["message_id"] === messageId
35:       && proof["stop_intent_id"] === phase.stop_intent_id && event["stop_intent_id"] === phase.stop_intent_id
36:       && event["session_id"] === bound.session_id && Array.isArray(event["action_ids"]) && event["action_ids"].includes(phase.action_id)
37:       && event["owner_generation"] === proof["owner_generation"] && Number.isSafeInteger(proof["owner_generation"]) && Number(proof["owner_generation"]) > 0;
38:   } catch { return false; }
39: }
40: function readProofSequence(value: string | null): number {
41:   try { const v = JSON.parse(value ?? "null")?.event_seq; return Number.isSafeInteger(v) ? v : -1; } catch { return -1; }
42: }

58: const terminal = new Set(["completed", "failed", "cancel_requested", "observation_ended"]);
59: const cleared = new Set(["cleared", "superseded", "cancelled_before_dispatch"]);
60: const POLICY_DIGEST = attentionDigest({ profile: "trusted_process_session_adoption_v1", completion_level: "observed_turn", automatic_unknown_retry: false });
61: 
62: export function engineeringSourceIdentity(sessionId: string, nativeSessionId: string): string {
63:   if (!nativeSessionId) throw Error("executor_not_qualified");
64:   return `v1:${attentionDigest({ session_id: sessionId, native_session_id: nativeSessionId })}`;
65: }
66: 
67: /** Synchronous domain operations execute under the daemon's existing owner
68:  * fence. No process, network, Adapter, or model calls occur in this store. */
69: export class EngineeringStore {
70:   private readonly permits = new WeakSet<EngineeringDispatchPermit>();
71:   constructor(readonly attention: AttentionStore, readonly owner: AttentionOwner, private readonly hostEvidence?: EngineeringHostEvidence) {}
72:   get db(): Database.Database { return this.attention.db; }
73:   getMission(missionId: string): EngineeringMission {
74:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_missions WHERE mission_id=?").get(missionId) as EngineeringMission | undefined;
75:     if (!row) throw Error("not_found"); return row;
76:   }
77:   getDispatch(messageId: string) { return getEngineeringDispatchPhase(this.db, messageId); }
78:   listReserved(limit = 100, after?: { created_at: number; action_id: string }) {
79:     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100) throw Error("invalid_limit");
80:     if (after && (!Number.isSafeInteger(after.created_at) || after.created_at < 0 || !after.action_id || after.action_id.length > 256)) throw Error("invalid_cursor");
81:     return this.db.prepare(`SELECT p.action_id,p.mission_id,m.owner_principal_id,p.client_session_id,m.session_id,p.message_id,p.created_at
82:       FROM attention_dispatch_phases p JOIN engineering_missions m ON m.mission_id=p.mission_id
83:       WHERE p.phase='reserved' AND m.stop_intent_id IS NULL AND m.state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')
84:       AND (p.created_at>? OR (p.created_at=? AND p.action_id>?))
85:       ORDER BY p.created_at,p.action_id LIMIT ?`).all(after?.created_at ?? -1, after?.created_at ?? -1, after?.action_id ?? "", limit) as Array<{ action_id: string; mission_id: string; owner_principal_id: string; client_session_id: string; session_id: string; message_id: string; created_at: number }>;
86:   }
87:   getReserved(actionId: string) {
88:     return this.db.prepare(`SELECT p.action_id,p.mission_id,m.owner_principal_id,p.client_session_id,m.session_id,p.message_id,p.created_at
89:       FROM attention_dispatch_phases p JOIN engineering_missions m ON m.mission_id=p.mission_id
90:       WHERE p.action_id=? AND p.phase='reserved' AND m.stop_intent_id IS NULL AND m.state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')`).get(actionId) as ReturnType<EngineeringStore["listReserved"]>[number] | undefined;
91:   }
92:   listActiveMissions(limit = 16, after?: { created_at: number; mission_id: string }) {
93:     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100 || (after && (!Number.isSafeInteger(after.created_at) || after.created_at < 0 || !after.mission_id))) throw Error("invalid_limit");
94:     return this.db.prepare(`SELECT mission_id,created_at FROM engineering_missions WHERE state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')
95:       AND (created_at>? OR (created_at=? AND mission_id>?)) ORDER BY created_at,mission_id LIMIT ?`).all(after?.created_at ?? -1, after?.created_at ?? -1, after?.mission_id ?? "", limit) as Array<{ mission_id: string; created_at: number }>;
96:   }
97:   private write<T>(fn: () => T): T {
98:     if (this.db.pragma("synchronous", { simple: true }) !== 2) throw Error("durability_required");
99:     return this.attention.write(this.owner, fn);
100:   }
101:   private access(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, scope: string, right: "read" | "write" | "stop" | "operator"): void {
102:     if (!principal.principal_id || !principal.client_session_id || !principal[`${right}_scopes`].includes(scope)) throw Error("permission_denied");
103:   }
104:   readableMission(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, missionId: string): EngineeringMission {
105:     const mission = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_missions WHERE mission_id=?").get(missionId) as EngineeringMission | undefined;
106:     if (!mission) throw Error("permission_denied");
107:     this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "read"); return mission;
108:   }
109:   private readable(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, missionId: string): EngineeringMission { return this.readableMission(principal, missionId); }
110:   private steer(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, mission: EngineeringMission): void {
111:     this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "read"); this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "write");
112:     if (mission.owner_principal_id !== principal.principal_id) throw Error("permission_denied");
113:   }
114:   private controlBudget(missionId: string): void {
115:     if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_requests WHERE mission_id=? AND operation NOT IN ('cancel','endObservation')").get(missionId) as { n: number }).n >= 256) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
116:   }
117:   private cas(mission: EngineeringMission, revision: number): void {
118:     if (mission.revision !== revision) throw Error("version_conflict");
119:     if (terminal.has(mission.state)) throw Error("mission_terminal");
120:   }
121:   private clock(now: number): void { if (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || now < 0) throw Error("invalid_clock"); }
122:   private event(missionId: string, kind: string, actor: string | null, payload: unknown, now: number): number {
123:     return Number(this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_events(mission_id,kind,actor,payload_json,created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)").run(missionId, kind, actor, JSON.stringify(payload), now).lastInsertRowid);
124:   }
125:   private bump(missionId: string, state: EngineeringMission["state"], now: number): EngineeringMission {
126:     this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(state, now, missionId);
127:     return this.getMission(missionId);
128:   }
129:   private request(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, requestId: string, operation: string, payload: unknown): StoredRequest | undefined {
130:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_requests WHERE principal_id=? AND client_session_id=? AND request_id=?").get(principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, requestId) as StoredRequest | undefined;
131:     if (row && (row.operation !== operation || row.payload_hash !== attentionDigest(payload))) throw Error("action_conflict");
132:     return row;
133:   }
134:   private saveRequest(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, requestId: string, operation: string, payload: unknown, missionId: string, result: unknown, now: number): void {
135:     this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_requests VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, requestId, operation, attentionDigest(payload), missionId, JSON.stringify(result), now);
136:   }
137:   private source(mission: Pick<EngineeringMission, "scope_id" | "task_id" | "run_id" | "session_id" | "source_instance_id" | "repo_path">) {
138:     const sessions = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path); const session = sessions.getSession(mission.session_id);
139:     const scope = this.attention.getScope(mission.scope_id);
140:     if (!session || !scope) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("unavailable", "source_unavailable");
141:     if (session.task_id !== mission.task_id || session.run_id !== mission.run_id || scope.task_id !== mission.task_id
142:       || !session.native_session_id || engineeringSourceIdentity(session.session_id, session.native_session_id) !== mission.source_instance_id
143:       || scope.repo_path !== mission.repo_path) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("identity", "source_identity_changed");
144:     let canonical: string;
145:     try { canonical = realpathSync(session.repo_path); }
146:     catch (error) {
147:       if (error && typeof error === "object" && "code" in error && ["ENOENT", "EIO", "ENOTDIR", "ESTALE", "ENODEV", "EBUSY", "EACCES", "EPERM"].includes(String(error.code))) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("unavailable", "repository_unavailable");
148:       throw error;
149:     }
150:     if (canonical !== mission.repo_path) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("identity", "repository_identity_changed");
151:     if (!this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM tasks t JOIN runs r ON r.task_id=t.task_id WHERE t.task_id=? AND r.run_id=? AND r.session_id=? AND r.agent_id=? AND t.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.tenant_id IS NULL").get(mission.task_id, mission.run_id, mission.session_id, session.agent_id)) throw new EngineeringSourceFault("identity", "source_scope_changed");
152:     return { session, snapshot: sessions.latestSnapshot(mission.session_id) };
153:   }
154:   private sourceIssue(missionId: string, reason: string, category: string, now: number): void {
155:     if (!this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND kind='source_observation_error' AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.reason')=?").get(missionId, reason)) this.event(missionId, "source_observation_error", null, { reason, category }, now);
156:   }
157:   private liveMessage(sessionId: string, messageId: string): boolean { return this.hostEvidence?.liveSend(sessionId) === messageId; }
158:   private ordinaryDecision(value: unknown): SessionDecision {
159:     const decision = SessionDecision.parse(value);
160:     const gate = decision.continuation?.gate_kind;
161:     if (gate === "risk_approval" || gate === "task_authorization") throw Error("signed_approval_required");
162:     if (gate === "phase_continue") throw Error("executor_not_qualified");
163:     return decision;
164:   }
165:   private ref(item: AttentionItem): EngineeringDecisionRef {
166:     return { kind: "session_decision", scope_id: item.scope_id, item_id: item.item_id, item_revision: item.revision, session_id: item.session_id,
167:       turn_id: item.turn_id, decision_id: item.decision_id, source_version: item.source_version, content_hash: item.content_hash };
168:   }
169:   private addWait(mission: EngineeringMission, snapshot: SessionSnapshot, now: number): void {
170:     if (!snapshot.turn_id) throw Error("source_unavailable");
171:     const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
172:     const itemId = attentionDigest([mission.scope_id, mission.session_id, snapshot.turn_id, decision.decision_id]);
173:     const existing = this.db.prepare("SELECT mission_id FROM engineering_waits WHERE item_id=?").get(itemId) as { mission_id: string } | undefined;
174:     if (existing) {
175:       if (existing.mission_id !== mission.mission_id) throw Error("authority_already_adopted");
176:       return;
177:     }
178:     const item = this.attention.projectDecision(this.owner, { scopeId: mission.scope_id, sessionId: mission.session_id, turnId: snapshot.turn_id, sourceVersion: snapshot.version, decision, now });
179:     const prior = this.db.prepare("SELECT mission_id FROM engineering_waits WHERE item_id=?").get(item.item_id) as { mission_id: string } | undefined;
180:     if (prior && prior.mission_id !== mission.mission_id) throw Error("authority_already_adopted");
181:     if (prior) return;
182:     if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=?").get(mission.mission_id) as { n: number }).n >= 64) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
183:     const waitId = randomUUID();
184:     this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_waits VALUES(?,?,?,1,?,'open',NULL,NULL,NULL,0,?,?)").run(waitId, mission.mission_id, item.item_id, JSON.stringify(this.ref(item)), now, now);
185:     this.event(mission.mission_id, "decision_observed", null, { wait_id: waitId, decision_ref: this.ref(item) }, now);
186:   }
187:   private waits(missionId: string): WaitRow[] { return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=? ORDER BY created_at,wait_id LIMIT 100").all(missionId) as WaitRow[]; }
188:   private wait(missionId: string, waitId: string): WaitRow {
189:     const wait = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE mission_id=? AND wait_id=?").get(missionId, waitId) as WaitRow | undefined;
190:     if (!wait) throw Error("not_found"); return wait;
191:   }
192:   private setWait(wait: WaitRow, state: WaitRow["state"], now: number): void {
193:     if (wait.state !== state) this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(state, now, wait.wait_id);
194:   }
195:   adopt(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringAdoptRequest, now = Date.now()) {
196:     const input = EngineeringAdoptRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now);
197:     this.access(principal, input.scope_id, "read");
198:     return this.write(() => {
199:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.request_id, "adopt", input);
200:       if (prior) return { mission: this.readable(principal, prior.mission_id), replay: true };
201:       this.access(principal, input.scope_id, "write");
202:       const sessions = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path); const session = sessions.getSession(input.session_id);
203:       if (!session || !session.resume_supported || !session.native_session_id) throw Error("executor_not_qualified");
204:       const scope = this.attention.getScope(input.scope_id);
205:       if (!scope || scope.task_id !== input.task_id) throw Error("scope_mismatch");
206:       const mission: EngineeringMission = { mission_id: randomUUID(), scope_id: input.scope_id, task_id: input.task_id, run_id: input.run_id, session_id: input.session_id,
207:         repo_path: scope.repo_path, source_instance_id: engineeringSourceIdentity(session.session_id, session.native_session_id), source_generation: 1,
208:         owner_principal_id: principal.principal_id, owner_client_session_id: principal.client_session_id, goal: input.goal, completion_level: "observed_turn", state: "awaiting_decision", revision: 1,
209:         stop_intent_id: null, created_at: now, updated_at: now, observed_at: now, policy_digest: POLICY_DIGEST, observation_proof_json: null };
210:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
211:       if (snapshot.state !== "awaiting_decision") throw Error("session_not_awaiting_decision");
212:       this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
213:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_missions WHERE session_id=? AND state NOT IN ('completed','failed','cancel_requested','observation_ended')").get(input.session_id)) throw Error("source_already_adopted");
214:       if ((this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_missions").get() as { n: number }).n >= 1000) throw Error("budget_exhausted");
215:       this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO engineering_missions VALUES(@mission_id,@scope_id,@task_id,@run_id,@session_id,@repo_path,@source_instance_id,@source_generation,@owner_principal_id,@owner_client_session_id,@goal,@completion_level,@state,@revision,@stop_intent_id,@created_at,@updated_at,@observed_at,@policy_digest,@observation_proof_json)`).run(mission);
216:       this.addWait(mission, snapshot, now); this.event(mission.mission_id, "session_adopted", principal.principal_id, { session_id: mission.session_id, source_instance_id: mission.source_instance_id, completion_level: "observed_turn" }, now);
217:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "adopt", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
218:       return { mission, replay: false };
219:     });
220:   }
221:   observe(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringObserveRequest, now = Date.now()) {
222:     const input = EngineeringObserveRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now);
223:     const readable = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
224:     return this.attention.write(this.owner, () => {
225:       const sourceStatus = this.refresh(readable.mission_id, now);
226:       const mission = this.getMission(input.mission_id);
227:       let snapshot: SessionSnapshot | null = null;
228:       const sessions = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path);
229:       if (sourceStatus !== "unavailable" && sessions.getSession(mission.session_id)) snapshot = sessions.latestSnapshot(mission.session_id);
230:       const waits = this.waits(mission.mission_id).map((wait): EngineeringWait => {
231:         const { decision_ref_json, ...rest } = wait;
232:         const decision_ref = JSON.parse(decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
233:         const item = this.attention.getItem(mission.scope_id, wait.item_id);
234:         if (!item) throw Error("source_unavailable");
235:         const result: EngineeringWait = { ...rest, decision_ref, decision: item.decision };
236:         if (!terminal.has(mission.state) && ["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) && principal.principal_id === mission.owner_principal_id && principal.write_scopes.includes(mission.scope_id)) {
237:           const token = randomBytes(32).toString("base64url"); const expires = now + 5 * 60_000;
238:           this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM engineering_interactions WHERE expires_at<?").run(now);
239:           this.db.prepare("DELETE FROM engineering_interactions WHERE mission_id=? AND rowid IN (SELECT rowid FROM engineering_interactions WHERE mission_id=? ORDER BY issued_at DESC,rowid DESC LIMIT -1 OFFSET 31)").run(mission.mission_id, mission.mission_id);
240:           this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_interactions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(attentionDigest(token), principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id, mission.mission_id, mission.revision, wait.wait_id, item.item_id, decision_ref.item_revision, decision_ref.content_hash, now, expires);
241:           result.interaction_token = token; result.token_expires_at = expires;
242:         }
243:         return result;
244:       });
245:       const limit = input.limit ?? 50; const rows = this.db.prepare("SELECT seq,kind,actor,payload_json,created_at FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND seq>? ORDER BY seq LIMIT ?").all(mission.mission_id, input.cursor ?? 0, limit + 1) as Array<{ seq: number; kind: string; actor: string | null; payload_json: string; created_at: number }>;
246:       const events = rows.slice(0, limit).map(({ payload_json, ...row }) => ({ ...row, payload: JSON.parse(payload_json) as unknown }));
247:       return { mission, waits, events, cursor: events.at(-1)?.seq ?? input.cursor ?? 0, has_more: rows.length > limit, source_status: sourceStatus,
248:         cost_evidence: { status: "unknown" as const, hard_cap_enforced: false as const, reason: "provider_usage_not_metered_by_adoption_pilot" as const },
249:         snapshot_digest: snapshot ? attentionDigest(snapshot) : null, source_version: snapshot?.version ?? null, turn_id: snapshot?.turn_id ?? null,
250:         observation_proof: mission.observation_proof_json ? JSON.parse(mission.observation_proof_json) as EngineeringObservationProof : null };
251:     });
252:   }
253:   decide(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringDecideRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringActionReceipt {
254:     const input = EngineeringDecideRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
255:     return this.write(() => {
256:       let mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
257:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.action_id, "decide", input);
258:       if (prior) return this.receipt(mission, input.action_id, true);
259:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
260:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id);
261:       if (!["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) || wait.action_id) throw Error("stale_decision");
262:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
263:       if (attentionDigest(ref) !== attentionDigest(input.decision_ref)) throw Error("stale_decision");
264:       const token = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_interactions WHERE token_hash=?").get(attentionDigest(input.interaction_token)) as TokenRow | undefined;
265:       if (!token || token.principal_id !== principal.principal_id || token.client_session_id !== principal.client_session_id || token.mission_id !== mission.mission_id
266:         || token.mission_revision !== input.if_revision || token.wait_id !== wait.wait_id || token.item_id !== ref.item_id || token.item_revision !== ref.item_revision || token.content_hash !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("interaction_mismatch");
267:       if (now < token.issued_at || now > token.expires_at) throw Error("interaction_expired");
268:       const { session, snapshot } = this.source(mission);
269:       if (session.version !== ref.source_version || snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || attentionDigest(SessionDecision.parse(snapshot.decision)) !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("stale_decision");
270:       const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
271:       let text: string;
272:       if (input.answer.kind === "choice") {
273:         const choiceId = input.answer.choice_id; const choice = decision.choices.find(c => c.choice_id === choiceId);
274:         if (!choice) throw Error("invalid_choice"); text = choice.label;
275:       } else {
276:         if (decision.choices.length > 0 || (decision.continuation && decision.continuation.gate_kind !== "external_input")) throw Error("explicit_choice_required");
277:         text = input.answer.text;
278:       }
279:       const reservation = new AttentionActions(this.attention, this.owner).reserve({ scope_id: mission.scope_id, item_id: ref.item_id, action_id: input.action_id, revision: ref.item_revision, content_hash: ref.content_hash, text, takeover: false }, now);
280:       if (reservation.replay || reservation.reservation_version !== ref.source_version + 1) throw Error("action_conflict");
281:       this.db.prepare(`INSERT INTO attention_dispatch_phases VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,1,'reserved',?,1,?,?,?,?,NULL,NULL,NULL,?,?)`).run(input.action_id, reservation.message_id, mission.mission_id, principal.principal_id, principal.client_session_id,
282:         mission.source_instance_id, ref.turn_id, ref.source_version, reservation.reservation_version, this.owner.generation, now, now);
283:       this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='answer_recorded',action_id=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(input.action_id, now, wait.wait_id);
284:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "answer_recorded", principal.principal_id, { action_id: input.action_id, message_id: reservation.message_id, wait_id: wait.wait_id, decision_ref: ref, client_session_id: principal.client_session_id, interaction_token_hash: attentionDigest(input.interaction_token), payload_digest: attentionDigest(input.answer) }, now);
285:       mission = this.bump(mission.mission_id, "answer_recorded", now);
286:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.action_id, "decide", input, mission.mission_id, { action_id: input.action_id }, now);
287:       return { mission, action_id: input.action_id, message_id: reservation.message_id, state: "reserved", replay: false, reservation_version: reservation.reservation_version };
288:     });
289:   }
290:   private receipt(mission: EngineeringMission, actionId: string, replay: boolean): EngineeringActionReceipt {
291:     const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT a.* FROM attention_actions a JOIN engineering_waits w ON w.action_id=a.action_id WHERE a.action_id=? AND w.mission_id=?").get(actionId, mission.mission_id) as ActionRow | undefined;
292:     if (!action) throw Error("not_found");
293:     const phase = this.getDispatch(action.message_id);
294:     const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(action.message_id);
295:     const resolved = this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(action.message_id);
296:     const state = phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch"
297:       ? isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.db, action.message_id) ? "cancelled_before_dispatch" : "needs_reconciliation"
298:       : resolved ? "abandoned_unknown"
299:       : phase?.phase === "reserved" ? action.state === "reserved" && message?.state === "accepted" ? "reserved" : "needs_reconciliation"
300:       : message?.state === "applied" ? "applied"
301:       : phase?.phase === "dispatch_committed" && message?.state === "accepted" && this.liveMessage(mission.session_id, action.message_id) ? "in_flight" : "uncertain";
302:     return { mission, action_id: actionId, message_id: action.message_id, state, replay, reservation_version: phase?.reservation_version ?? 0 };
303:   }
304:   commitDispatch(actionId: string, principal: EngineeringPrincipal, now = Date.now()) {
305:     this.clock(now);
306:     return this.write(() => {
307:       const prepared = this.preflightDispatch(actionId, principal);
308:       const changed = this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_dispatch_phases SET phase='dispatch_committed',committed_by_generation=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND phase='reserved'").run(this.owner.generation, now, actionId);
309:       if (changed.changes !== 1) throw Error("dispatch_already_committed");
310:       const phase = this.getDispatch(prepared.message_id)!;
311:       this.event(phase.mission_id, "dispatch_committed", principal.principal_id, { action_id: actionId, message_id: prepared.message_id, daemon_generation: this.owner.generation }, now);
312:       const permit: EngineeringDispatchPermit = Object.freeze({ message_id: prepared.message_id, daemon_generation: this.owner.generation }); this.permits.add(permit);
313:       return { permit, ...prepared };
314:     });
315:   }
316:   /** Cheap read-only qualification before asynchronous admission. commitDispatch
317:    * repeats every predicate inside the owner-fenced FULL write transaction. */
318:   preflightDispatch(actionId: string, principal: EngineeringPrincipal) {
319:       this.attention.assertOwner(this.owner);
320:       if (this.db.pragma("synchronous", { simple: true }) !== 2) throw Error("durability_required");
321:       const phase = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE action_id=?").get(actionId) as ReturnType<EngineeringStore["getDispatch"]>;
322:       if (!phase) throw Error("dispatch_gate_required");
323:       const mission = this.readable(principal, phase.mission_id); this.steer(principal, mission);
324:       if (phase.principal_id !== principal.principal_id || phase.client_session_id !== principal.client_session_id) throw Error("permission_denied");
325:       if (phase.phase !== "reserved") throw Error(phase.phase === "dispatch_committed" ? "dispatch_already_committed" : "cancelled_original_answer");
326:       if (terminal.has(mission.state) || mission.stop_intent_id) throw Error("cancelled_original_answer");
327:       if (mission.policy_digest !== POLICY_DIGEST) throw Error("policy_changed");
328:       const { session, snapshot } = this.source(mission);
329:       const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(actionId) as ActionRow | undefined;
330:       const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(phase.message_id);
331:       const wait = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM engineering_waits WHERE action_id=? AND mission_id=?").get(actionId, mission.mission_id) as WaitRow | undefined;
332:       if (!action || action.state !== "reserved" || action.scope_id !== mission.scope_id || action.message_id !== phase.message_id || !message || message.state !== "accepted"
333:         || message.session_id !== mission.session_id || message.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || !wait || wait.state !== "answer_recorded" || wait.item_id !== action.item_id
334:         || phase.protocol_version !== 1 || phase.source_instance_id !== mission.source_instance_id || phase.source_generation !== mission.source_generation
335:         || session.version !== phase.reservation_version || snapshot.turn_id !== phase.original_turn_id || snapshot.state !== "awaiting_decision") throw Error("needs_reconciliation");
336:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(phase.message_id)) throw Error("receipt_resolved");
337:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
338:       const decision = this.ordinaryDecision(snapshot.decision);
339:       if (attentionDigest(decision) !== ref.content_hash || ref.source_version !== phase.source_version) throw Error("source_changed");
340:       const payload = JSON.parse(message.payload_json) as { text?: unknown };
341:       if (typeof payload.text !== "string" || action.payload_hash !== attentionDigest({ scope_id: mission.scope_id, item_id: ref.item_id, action_id: actionId, revision: ref.item_revision, content_hash: ref.content_hash, text: payload.text, takeover: false })) throw Error("payload_changed");
342:       return { session_id: mission.session_id, message_id: message.message_id, turn_id: phase.original_turn_id, reservation_version: phase.reservation_version, text: payload.text, action_id: actionId };
343:   }
344:   recordDispatchBlocked(actionId: string, reason: string, now = Date.now()): void {
345:     if (!/^[a-z_]{1,64}$/.test(reason)) throw Error("invalid_block_reason");
346:     this.write(() => {
347:       const phase = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE action_id=?").get(actionId) as ReturnType<EngineeringStore["getDispatch"]>;
348:       if (!phase || phase.phase !== "reserved") return;
349:       const count = this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND kind='dispatch_blocked' AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.action_id')=?").get(phase.mission_id, actionId) as { n: number };
350:       if (count.n >= 16 || this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND kind='dispatch_blocked' AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.action_id')=? AND json_extract(payload_json,'$.reason')=?").get(phase.mission_id, actionId, reason)) return;
351:       this.event(phase.mission_id, "dispatch_blocked", null, { action_id: actionId, reason }, now);
352:     });
353:   }
354:   consumeDispatchPermit(permit: EngineeringDispatchPermit | undefined, messageId: string): void {
355:     this.attention.assertOwner(this.owner);
356:     if (!permit || permit.message_id !== messageId || permit.daemon_generation !== this.owner.generation || !this.permits.delete(permit)) throw Error("dispatch_gate_required");
357:   }
358:   defer(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringDeferRequest, now = Date.now()) {
359:     const input = EngineeringDeferRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
360:     return this.write(() => {
361:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
362:       if (this.request(principal, input.request_id, "defer", input)) return { mission, replay: true };
363:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
364:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
365:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id); const revisit = Date.parse(input.revisit_at);
366:       if (wait.revision !== input.wait_revision || !["open", "deferred"].includes(wait.state) || wait.action_id) throw Error("stale_decision");
367:       const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
368:       if (snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || snapshot.version !== ref.source_version || attentionDigest(SessionDecision.parse(snapshot.decision)) !== ref.content_hash) throw Error("stale_decision");
369:       if (revisit <= now || revisit > now + 7 * 86400_000) throw Error("invalid_revisit_at");
370:       this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='deferred',revision=revision+1,revisit_at=?,reason=?,reminder_sent=0,updated_at=? WHERE wait_id=?").run(revisit, input.reason, now, wait.wait_id);
371:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "decision_deferred", principal.principal_id, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, revisit_at: input.revisit_at, reason: input.reason }, now);
372:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, mission.state, now); this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "defer", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
373:       return { mission: updated, replay: false };
374:     });
375:   }
376:   cancel(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringCancelRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringStopResult { return this.end(principal, EngineeringCancelRequest.parse(value), false, now); }
377:   endObservation(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringEndObservationRequest, now = Date.now()): EngineeringStopResult { return this.end(principal, EngineeringEndObservationRequest.parse(value), true, now); }
378:   private end(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, input: EngineeringCancelRequest, observationOnly: boolean, now: number): EngineeringStopResult {
379:     this.clock(now); this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
380:     return this.write(() => {
381:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id); const operation = observationOnly ? "endObservation" : "cancel";
382:       const prior = this.request(principal, input.request_id, operation, input);
383:       if (prior) return { ...(JSON.parse(prior.response_json) as EngineeringStopResult), mission, replay: true };
384:       const isOwner = mission.owner_principal_id === principal.principal_id;
385:       if (observationOnly) {
386:         if (!isOwner) { this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "operator"); this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "stop"); }
387:       } else {
388:         this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "stop");
389:         if (!isOwner) this.access(principal, mission.scope_id, "operator");
390:       }
391:       if (mission.revision !== input.if_revision) throw Error("version_conflict");
392:       const phases = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_dispatch_phases WHERE mission_id=?").all(mission.mission_id) as NonNullable<ReturnType<EngineeringStore["getDispatch"]>>[];
393:       const committedIds = phases.filter(phase => phase.phase === "dispatch_committed"
394:         && new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(phase.message_id)?.state !== "applied").map(phase => phase.message_id);
395:       if (mission.state === "completed" || mission.state === "failed") throw Error("mission_terminal");
396:       if (terminal.has(mission.state) && (observationOnly || committedIds.length === 0)) throw Error("mission_terminal");
397:       // Retain the first durable intent. A bounded later attempt may request stop
398:       // only for this Mission's unresolved committed messages, never a later send.
399:       const stopAttempts = (this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM engineering_requests WHERE mission_id=? AND operation='cancel'").get(mission.mission_id) as { n: number }).n;
400:       if (!observationOnly && stopAttempts >= 8) throw Error("stop_attempt_budget_exhausted");
401:       const intent = mission.stop_intent_id ?? randomUUID();
402:       const kind = observationOnly ? "observation_ended" : stopAttempts > 0 ? "stop_retry_requested" : "stop_requested";
403:       const eventSeq = this.event(mission.mission_id, kind, principal.principal_id, { stop_intent_id: intent, request_id: input.request_id, run_id: mission.run_id, session_id: mission.session_id, action_ids: phases.map(p => p.action_id), owner_generation: this.owner.generation, reason: input.reason ?? null }, now);
404:       if (mission.stop_intent_id === null) this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET stop_intent_id=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(intent, mission.mission_id);
405:       for (const phase of phases) {
406:         if (phase.phase !== "reserved") continue;
407:         const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT state,message_id FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(phase.action_id) as { state: string; message_id: string } | undefined;
408:         const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(phase.message_id);
409:         if (!action || action.state !== "reserved" || action.message_id !== phase.message_id || message?.state !== "accepted" || message.session_id !== mission.session_id
410:           || this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?").get(phase.message_id)) continue;
411:         const proof = { version: 1, prior_phase: "reserved", action_id: phase.action_id, message_id: phase.message_id, stop_intent_id: intent, event_seq: eventSeq, owner_generation: this.owner.generation };
412:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_dispatch_phases SET phase='cancelled_before_dispatch',stop_intent_id=?,proof_json=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND phase='reserved'").run(intent, JSON.stringify(proof), now, phase.action_id);
413:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_actions SET state='cancelled_before_dispatch',updated_at=? WHERE action_id=?").run(now, phase.action_id);
414:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_cancelled',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?)").run(now, phase.action_id);
415:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET state='cancelled_before_dispatch',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=?").run(now, phase.action_id);
416:       }
417:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, observationOnly ? "observation_ended" : "cancel_requested", now);
418:       const result: EngineeringStopResult = { mission: updated, replay: false, stop_intent_id: intent, run_id: mission.run_id, session_id: mission.session_id, action_ids: phases.map(p => p.action_id), committed_message_ids: committedIds };
419:       this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, operation, input, mission.mission_id, result, now); return result;
420:     });
421:   }
422:   recordStopReceipt(missionId: string, intentId: string, status: "stop_requested" | "stop_unknown", evidence: unknown, now = Date.now()): void {
423:     this.write(() => {
424:       const mission = this.getMission(missionId);
425:       if (mission.stop_intent_id !== intentId) throw Error("stop_intent_changed");
426:       const kind = status === "stop_unknown" ? "stop_unknown" : "stop_request_acknowledged";
427:       const payload = { stop_intent_id: intentId, evidence };
428:       if (!this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM engineering_events WHERE mission_id=? AND kind=? AND payload_json=?").get(missionId, kind, JSON.stringify(payload))) this.event(missionId, kind, null, payload, now);
429:     });
430:   }
431:   reconcile(principal: EngineeringPrincipal, value: EngineeringReconcileRequest, now = Date.now()) {
432:     const input = EngineeringReconcileRequest.parse(value); this.clock(now); const initial = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
433:     if (input.kind === "query_action") return this.attention.write(this.owner, () => this.receipt(initial, input.action_id, true));
434:     return this.write(() => {
435:       const mission = this.readable(principal, input.mission_id);
436:       if (this.request(principal, input.request_id, "dispose_wait", input)) return { mission, replay: true };
437:       this.steer(principal, mission); this.cas(mission, input.if_revision); this.controlBudget(mission.mission_id);
438:       const wait = this.wait(mission.mission_id, input.wait_id);
439:       if (wait.revision !== input.wait_revision || wait.action_id || cleared.has(wait.state)) throw Error("receipt_requires_reconciliation");
440:       if (this.db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_actions WHERE item_id=?").get(wait.item_id)) throw Error("receipt_requires_reconciliation");
441:       const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
442:       if (snapshot.version !== input.observed_source_version || snapshot.turn_id !== input.observed_turn_id || attentionDigest(snapshot) !== input.observed_snapshot_digest) throw Error("source_changed");
443:       const item = this.attention.getItem(mission.scope_id, wait.item_id); if (!item) throw Error("source_unavailable"); this.ordinaryDecision(item.decision);
444:       this.setWait(wait, "superseded", now);
445:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "steering_recorded", principal.principal_id, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, disposition: input.disposition, observed_snapshot_digest: input.observed_snapshot_digest, reason: input.reason, delivery_verified: false }, now);
446:       const updated = this.bump(mission.mission_id, "observing", now); this.saveRequest(principal, input.request_id, "dispose_wait", input, mission.mission_id, {}, now);
447:       return { mission: updated, replay: false };
448:     });
449:   }
450:   /** Must be called from the same transaction that marks the original message
451:    * applied and writes its new Session turn. This is observation, not delivery. */
452:   recordCompletion(messageId: string, completedTurnId: string, sessionVersion: number, now = Date.now()): void {
453:     if (!this.db.inTransaction) throw Error("completion_transaction_required");
454:     this.attention.assertOwner(this.owner);
455:     const phase = this.getDispatch(messageId); if (!phase) return;
456:     if (phase.phase !== "dispatch_committed") throw Error("dispatch_inconsistent");
457:     const mission = this.getMission(phase.mission_id); const { snapshot } = this.source(mission);
458:     const message = new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(messageId);
459:     if (message?.state !== "applied" || snapshot.turn_id !== completedTurnId || snapshot.version !== sessionVersion || sessionVersion !== phase.reservation_version + 1) throw Error("completion_mismatch");
460:     const proof: EngineeringObservationProof = { kind: "observed_turn", action_id: phase.action_id, message_id: messageId, source_instance_id: phase.source_instance_id, source_generation: phase.source_generation, completed_turn_id: completedTurnId, session_version: sessionVersion, output_sha256: attentionDigest(snapshot.assistant_text) };
461:     const prior = this.db.prepare("SELECT proof_json FROM engineering_completions WHERE action_id=?").get(phase.action_id) as { proof_json: string } | undefined;
462:     if (prior && prior.proof_json !== JSON.stringify(proof)) throw Error("completion_mismatch");
463:     if (!prior) {
464:       this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO engineering_completions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?)").run(phase.action_id, messageId, mission.mission_id, completedTurnId, sessionVersion, JSON.stringify(proof), now);
465:       this.event(mission.mission_id, "answer_applied", null, proof, now);
466:     }
467:     this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_actions SET state='applied',updated_at=? WHERE action_id=? AND message_id=? AND state<>'applied'").run(now, phase.action_id, messageId);
468:     this.db.prepare("UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_applied',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=? AND message_id=?) AND state<>'answer_applied'").run(now, phase.action_id, messageId);
469:     this.refresh(mission.mission_id, now);
470:   }
471:   /** Deterministic bounded projection; empty polls neither invoke a model nor
472:    * increment the Mission's intent revision. */
473:   refresh(missionId: string, now = Date.now()): SourceStatus {
474:     this.clock(now);
475:     return this.attention.write(this.owner, () => {
476:       let mission = this.getMission(missionId); if (terminal.has(mission.state)) return "not_observing";
477:       let snapshot: SessionSnapshot;
478:       try { snapshot = this.source(mission).snapshot; }
479:       catch (error) {
480:         if (!(error instanceof EngineeringSourceFault)) throw error;
481:         this.sourceIssue(missionId, error.reason, error.category, now);
482:         if (error.category === "unavailable") return "unavailable";
483:         for (const wait of this.waits(missionId)) if (!cleared.has(wait.state)) this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now);
484:         if (mission.state !== "needs_reconciliation") this.bump(missionId, "needs_reconciliation", now); return "identity_mismatch";
485:       }
486:       let uncertain = false;
487:       for (const wait of this.waits(missionId)) {
488:         if (cleared.has(wait.state)) continue;
489:         if (wait.state === "needs_reconciliation") { uncertain = true; continue; }
490:         if (wait.state === "deferred" && wait.revisit_at !== null && now >= wait.revisit_at && now >= wait.updated_at && wait.reminder_sent === 0) {
491:           this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_waits SET reminder_sent=1 WHERE wait_id=? AND reminder_sent=0").run(wait.wait_id);
492:           this.event(missionId, "decision_reminder_due", null, { wait_id: wait.wait_id, revisit_at: wait.revisit_at, reminder_budget: 1 }, now);
493:         }
494:         if (wait.action_id) {
495:           const action = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?").get(wait.action_id) as ActionRow | undefined;
496:           if (!action) { this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now); uncertain = true; continue; }
497:           const phase = this.getDispatch(action.message_id); const completion = this.db.prepare("SELECT proof_json FROM engineering_completions WHERE action_id=?").get(wait.action_id) as { proof_json: string } | undefined;
498:           if (completion && new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(action.message_id)?.state === "applied") { this.setWait(wait, "cleared", now); continue; }
499:           if (phase?.phase === "cancelled_before_dispatch" && isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(this.db, action.message_id)) { this.setWait(wait, "cancelled_before_dispatch", now); continue; }
500:           if (phase?.phase === "dispatch_committed" && new SessionRepository(this.db, this.attention.path).getMessage(action.message_id)?.state === "accepted" && this.liveMessage(mission.session_id, action.message_id)) {
501:             this.setWait(wait, "answer_recorded", now); continue;
502:           }
503:           const receipt = new AttentionActions(this.attention, this.owner).reconcile(action, now);
504:           if (receipt.state === "reserved") continue;
505:           this.setWait(wait, "delivery_unknown", now); uncertain = true;
506:         } else {
507:           const ref = JSON.parse(wait.decision_ref_json) as EngineeringDecisionRef;
508:           const parsed = SessionDecision.safeParse(snapshot.decision);
509:           if (snapshot.turn_id !== ref.turn_id || snapshot.version !== ref.source_version || !parsed.success || attentionDigest(parsed.data) !== ref.content_hash) {
510:             this.setWait(wait, "needs_reconciliation", now); uncertain = true; this.sourceIssue(missionId, "decision_source_changed", "decision", now);
511:           }
512:         }
513:       }
514:       if (snapshot.state === "awaiting_decision" && snapshot.decision) {
515:         try { this.addWait(mission, snapshot, now); }
516:         catch (error) {
517:           const reason = error instanceof Error && error.name === "ZodError" ? "invalid_decision" : error instanceof Error ? error.message : "";
518:           if (!["signed_approval_required", "executor_not_qualified", "decision_content_conflict", "authority_already_adopted", "budget_exhausted", "source_unavailable", "invalid_decision"].includes(reason)) throw error;
519:           uncertain = true; this.sourceIssue(missionId, reason, "decision", now);
520:         }
521:       }
522:       const waits = this.waits(missionId); const unclosed = waits.some(wait => !cleared.has(wait.state));
523:       const completion = this.db.prepare("SELECT proof_json FROM engineering_completions WHERE mission_id=? AND completed_turn_id=? AND session_version=?").get(missionId, snapshot.turn_id, snapshot.version) as { proof_json: string } | undefined;
524:       if (!uncertain && !unclosed && !mission.stop_intent_id && completion && snapshot.decision === null && ["idle", "completed"].includes(snapshot.state)) {
525:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET observation_proof_json=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(completion.proof_json, missionId);
526:         mission = this.bump(missionId, "completed", now); this.event(missionId, "observation_completed", null, JSON.parse(completion.proof_json), now);
527:       } else {
528:         const state = uncertain ? "needs_reconciliation" : waits.some(w => ["open", "deferred"].includes(w.state)) ? "awaiting_decision" : waits.some(w => w.state === "answer_recorded") ? "answer_recorded" : "observing";
529:         if (mission.state !== state) this.bump(missionId, state, now);
530:       }
531:       // FULL durability applies to the shared connection. Empty one-second
532:       // watchdog ticks must not issue a persistent heartbeat every time.
533:       if (now >= mission.observed_at + 60_000) this.db.prepare("UPDATE engineering_missions SET observed_at=? WHERE mission_id=?").run(now, missionId);
534:       return "available";
535:     });
536:   }
537: }

79: function parseSession(row: RawSessionRow): AgentSessionRowType {
