83: function canonicalJson(value: unknown): string {
84:   if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
85:   if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalJson).join(",")}]`;
86:   const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
87:     .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right));
88:   return `{${entries.map(([key, item]) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalJson(item)}`).join(",")}}`;
89: }
90: 
91: export class SessionRepository {
92:   constructor(
93:     private readonly db: Database.Database,
94:     private readonly path: string,
95:   ) {}
96: 
97:   createSession(row: AgentSessionRowType): void {
98:     const parsed = AgentSessionRow.parse(row);
99:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
100:       db.prepare(`
101:         INSERT INTO agent_sessions(
102:           session_id,task_id,run_id,agent_id,request_sha256,native_session_id,
103:           repo_path,state,resume_supported,version,updated_at
104:         ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
105:       `).run(
106:         parsed.session_id, parsed.task_id, parsed.run_id, parsed.agent_id,
107:         parsed.request_sha256, parsed.native_session_id, parsed.repo_path, parsed.state,
108:         parsed.resume_supported ? 1 : 0, parsed.version, parsed.updated_at,
109:       );
110:     });
111:   }
112: 
113:   getSession(sessionId: string): AgentSessionRowType | undefined {
114:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
115:       .get(sessionId) as RawSessionRow | undefined;
116:     return row ? parseSession(row) : undefined;
117:   }
118: 
119:   recordNativeSession(
120:     sessionId: string,
121:     nativeSessionId: string,
122:     expectedVersion: number,
123:     updatedAt: number,
124:   ): AgentSessionRowType {
125:     return this.updateSession(
126:       sessionId,
127:       expectedVersion,
128:       updatedAt,
129:       "native_session_id = ?, state = 'idle'",
130:       [nativeSessionId],
131:     );
132:   }
133: 
134:   setState(
135:     sessionId: string,
136:     state: AgentSessionStateType,
137:     expectedVersion: number,
138:     updatedAt: number,
139:   ): AgentSessionRowType {
140:     AgentSessionState.parse(state);
141:     return this.updateSession(sessionId, expectedVersion, updatedAt, "state = ?", [state]);
142:   }
143: 
144:   appendTurn(input: SessionTurnInput): void {
145:     const { decision, ...stored } = input;
146:     const row = SessionTurnRow.parse({
147:       ...stored,
148:       decision_json: decision === null ? null : canonicalJson(decision),
149:     });
150:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
151:       db.prepare(`
152:         INSERT INTO session_turns(
153:           turn_id,session_id,ordinal,state,assistant_text,decision_json,created_at,updated_at
154:         ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
155:       `).run(
156:         row.turn_id, row.session_id, row.ordinal, row.state, row.assistant_text,
157:         row.decision_json, row.created_at, row.updated_at,
158:       );
159:     });
160:   }
161: 
162:   applyInbound(
163:     input: SessionMessageInput,
164:     expectedVersion: number,
165:     appliedAt: number,
166:   ): ApplyMessageResult {
167:     const { payload, ...stored } = input;
168:     const payloadJson = canonicalJson(payload);
169:     const row = SessionMessageRow.parse({
170:       ...stored,
171:       payload_json: payloadJson,
172:       applied_at: input.applied_at ?? null,
173:     });
174: 
175:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
176:       const existing = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM session_messages WHERE message_id = ?")
177:         .get(row.message_id) as SessionMessageRowType | undefined;
178:       if (existing) {
179:         if (
180:           existing.session_id !== row.session_id
181:           || existing.turn_id !== row.turn_id
182:           || existing.kind !== row.kind
183:           || existing.payload_json !== payloadJson
184:         ) {
185:           throw new SessionMessageIdConflict(row.message_id);
186:         }
187:         const session = this.readSession(db, row.session_id);
188:         return { outcome: "duplicate", session_version: session.version };
189:       }
190: 
191:       const result = db.prepare(`
192:         UPDATE agent_sessions
193:         SET version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
194:         WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
195:       `).run(appliedAt, row.session_id, expectedVersion);
196:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
197:         const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
198:           .get(row.session_id) as { version: number } | undefined;
199:         throw new SessionVersionConflict(row.session_id, expectedVersion, actual?.version ?? null);
200:       }
201: 
202:       db.prepare(`
203:         INSERT INTO session_messages(
204:           message_id,session_id,turn_id,direction,kind,payload_json,state,created_at,applied_at
205:         ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
206:       `).run(
207:         row.message_id, row.session_id, row.turn_id, row.direction, row.kind,
208:         payloadJson, row.state, row.created_at, row.applied_at,
209:       );
210:       return { outcome: "applied", session_version: expectedVersion + 1 };
211:     });
212:   }
213: 
214:   markMessageApplied(messageId: string, appliedAt: number): void {
215:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
216:       const result = db.prepare(`
217:         UPDATE session_messages
218:         SET state = 'applied', applied_at = ?
219:         WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
220:       `).run(appliedAt, messageId);
221:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
222:         throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not accepted`);
223:       }
224:     });
225:   }
226: 
227:   markMessageFailed(messageId: string): void {
228:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
229:       const result = db.prepare(`
230:         UPDATE session_messages
231:         SET state = 'failed', applied_at = NULL
232:         WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
233:       `).run(messageId);
234:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
235:         throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not accepted`);
236:       }
237:     });
238:   }
239: 
240:   retryFailedMessage(messageId: string): void {
241:     withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
242:       const result = db.prepare(`
243:         UPDATE session_messages
244:         SET state = 'accepted', applied_at = NULL
245:         WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'failed'
246:       `).run(messageId);
247:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
248:         throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not failed`);
249:       }
250:     });
251:   }
252: 
253:   completeInboundTurn(input: CompleteInboundTurnInput): AgentSessionRowType {
254:     AgentSessionState.parse(input.turn.state);
255:     const { decision, ...storedTurn } = input.turn;
256:     const turn = SessionTurnRow.parse({
257:       ...storedTurn,
258:       decision_json: decision === null ? null : canonicalJson(decision),
259:     });
260: 
261:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
262:       const message = db.prepare(`
263:         SELECT session_id, state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id = ?
264:       `).get(input.message_id) as {
265:         session_id: string;
266:         state: SessionMessageRowType["state"];
267:       } | undefined;
268:       if (
269:         message === undefined
270:         || message.session_id !== turn.session_id
271:         || message.state !== "accepted"
272:       ) {
273:         throw new Error(
274:           `message ${input.message_id} is missing, belongs to another session, or is not accepted`,
275:         );
276:       }
277: 
278:       const updated = db.prepare(`
279:         UPDATE agent_sessions
280:         SET state = ?, version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
281:         WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
282:       `).run(
283:         turn.state,
284:         input.applied_at,
285:         turn.session_id,
286:         input.expected_version,
287:       );
288:       if (updated.changes !== 1) {
289:         const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
290:           .get(turn.session_id) as { version: number } | undefined;
291:         throw new SessionVersionConflict(
292:           turn.session_id,
293:           input.expected_version,
294:           actual?.version ?? null,
295:         );
296:       }
297: 
298:       db.prepare(`
299:         INSERT INTO session_turns(
300:           turn_id,session_id,ordinal,state,assistant_text,decision_json,created_at,updated_at
301:         ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
302:       `).run(
303:         turn.turn_id,
304:         turn.session_id,
305:         turn.ordinal,
306:         turn.state,
307:         turn.assistant_text,
308:         turn.decision_json,
309:         turn.created_at,
310:         turn.updated_at,
311:       );
312: 
313:       const applied = db.prepare(`
314:         UPDATE session_messages
315:         SET state = 'applied', applied_at = ?
316:         WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
317:       `).run(input.applied_at, input.message_id);
318:       if (applied.changes !== 1) {
319:         throw new Error(`message ${input.message_id} could not be marked applied`);
320:       }
321:       return this.readSession(db, turn.session_id);
322:     });
323:   }
324: 
325:   getMessage(messageId: string): SessionMessageRowType | undefined {
326:     const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM session_messages WHERE message_id = ?")
327:       .get(messageId) as SessionMessageRowType | undefined;
328:     return row ? SessionMessageRow.parse(row) : undefined;
329:   }
330: 
331:   latestSnapshot(sessionId: string): SessionSnapshot {
332:     const session = this.readSession(this.db, sessionId);
333:     const turn = this.db.prepare(`
334:       SELECT * FROM session_turns WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY ordinal DESC LIMIT 1
335:     `).get(sessionId) as SessionTurnRowType | undefined;
336:     const parsedTurn = turn ? SessionTurnRow.parse(turn) : undefined;
337:     return {
338:       session_id: session.session_id,
339:       task_id: session.task_id,
340:       run_id: session.run_id,
341:       agent_id: session.agent_id,
342:       runtime_id: session.agent_id,
343:       state: session.state,
344:       version: session.version,
345:       turn_id: parsedTurn?.turn_id ?? null,
346:       assistant_text: parsedTurn?.assistant_text ?? "",
347:       decision: parsedTurn?.decision_json ? JSON.parse(parsedTurn.decision_json) : null,
348:       updated_at: session.updated_at,
349:     };
350:   }
351: 
352:   listSessions(filter: SessionFilter = {}): AgentSessionRowType[] {
353:     const where: string[] = [];
354:     const values: Array<string | number> = [];
355:     if (filter.agent_id !== undefined) {
356:       where.push("agent_id = ?");
357:       values.push(filter.agent_id);
358:     }
359:     if (filter.state !== undefined) {
360:       AgentSessionState.parse(filter.state);
361:       where.push("state = ?");
362:       values.push(filter.state);
363:     }
364:     if (filter.task_id !== undefined) {
365:       where.push("task_id = ?");
366:       values.push(filter.task_id);
367:     }
368:     const limit = filter.limit ?? 200;
369:     if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 200) {
370:       throw new Error("session list limit must be an integer between 1 and 200");
371:     }
372:     values.push(limit);
373:     const sql = `SELECT * FROM agent_sessions${where.length > 0 ? ` WHERE ${where.join(" AND ")}` : ""} ORDER BY updated_at DESC, session_id LIMIT ?`;
374:     return (this.db.prepare(sql).all(...values) as RawSessionRow[]).map(parseSession);
375:   }
376: 
377:   private updateSession(
378:     sessionId: string,
379:     expectedVersion: number,
380:     updatedAt: number,
381:     assignment: string,
382:     values: unknown[],
383:   ): AgentSessionRowType {
384:     return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
385:       const result = db.prepare(`
386:         UPDATE agent_sessions
387:         SET ${assignment}, version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
388:         WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
389:       `).run(...values, updatedAt, sessionId, expectedVersion);
390:       if (result.changes !== 1) {
391:         const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
392:           .get(sessionId) as { version: number } | undefined;
393:         throw new SessionVersionConflict(sessionId, expectedVersion, actual?.version ?? null);
394:       }
395:       return this.readSession(db, sessionId);
396:     });
397:   }
398: 
399:   private readSession(db: Database.Database, sessionId: string): AgentSessionRowType {
400:     const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
401:       .get(sessionId) as RawSessionRow | undefined;
402:     if (!row) throw new SessionVersionConflict(sessionId, 0, null);
403:     return parseSession(row);
404:   }
405: }

761: async function handleSessionSend(
762:   ctx: DaemonContext,
763:   req: DaemonRequest,
764: ): Promise<SingleResponse> {
765:   const p = parseSessionSendParams(req.params);
766:   if (p instanceof Error) return singleErr(req.id, "bad_request", p.message);
767:   // Completed receipt replay remains read-only while drained; no new/retried
768:   // instruction may reserve a message or invoke an adapter during an upgrade.
769:   if (ctx.draining && ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id)?.state !== "applied" && !isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(ctx.db, p.message_id)) {
770:     return singleErr(req.id, "draining", "daemon is not accepting session instructions");
771:   }
772: 
773:   const session = ctx.sessions.getSession(p.session_id);
774:   if (session === undefined) return singleErr(req.id, "not_found", "session not found");
775:   if (p.runtime_id !== session.agent_id) {
776:     return singleErr(req.id, "runtime_mismatch", "session belongs to another runtime");
777:   }
778:   // Idempotency wins over moving state: a retry may arrive after the first
779:   // delivery already appended a newer turn or completed the conversation.
780:   // Let the repository compare the complete canonical envelope before any
781:   // current-turn/current-state guards reject it.
782:   const existingMessage = ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id);
783:   if (existingMessage !== undefined) {
784:     try {
785:       const replay = ctx.sessions.applyInbound({
786:         message_id: p.message_id,
787:         session_id: p.session_id,
788:         turn_id: p.turn_id,
789:         direction: "inbound",
790:         kind: "instruction.send",
791:         payload: { text: p.text, ...(p.requested_by === undefined ? {} : { requested_by: p.requested_by }) },
792:         state: "accepted",
793:         created_at: Date.now() / 1000,
794:       }, p.expected_version, Date.now() / 1000);
795:       if (isEngineeringCancelledBeforeDispatch(ctx.db, p.message_id)) {
796:         return singleErr(req.id, "cancelled_original_answer", "The original answer was cancelled before dispatch. It cannot be sent again.");
797:       }
798:       if (existingMessage.state === "applied") {
799:         return singleOk(req.id, { state: replay.outcome, session_version: replay.session_version });
800:       }
801:       if (replay.session_version !== p.expected_version + 1) {
802:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "accepted message cannot be recovered safely");
803:       }
804:       if (existingMessage.state === "accepted") {
805:         return ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)
806:           ? singleErr(req.id, "session_busy", "session is already applying another instruction")
807:           : singleErr(
808:               req.id,
809:               "delivery_uncertain",
810:               "the previous delivery may have reached the agent; inspect the session before using a new message_id",
811:             );
812:       }
813:       if (existingMessage.state !== "failed") {
814:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "reserved message is not retryable");
815:       }
816:       return singleErr(req.id, "delivery_uncertain", "Failed execution may have had side effects; reconcile the existing receipt before retrying");
817:     } catch (err) {
818:       if (err instanceof SessionMessageIdConflict) {
819:         return singleErr(req.id, "message_id_conflict", "message_id was already used for different content");
820:       }
821:       throw err;
822:     }
823:   }
824:   if (TERMINAL_SESSION_STATES.has(session.state)) {
825:     return singleErr(req.id, "session_terminal", `session state=${session.state}`);
826:   }
827:   if (!session.resume_supported || session.native_session_id === null) {
828:     return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "session cannot be resumed");
829:   }
830:   const snapshot = ctx.sessions.latestSnapshot(p.session_id);
831:   if (snapshot.turn_id !== p.turn_id) {
832:     return singleErr(req.id, "turn_conflict", "message does not target the current turn");
833:   }
834:   const adapter = ctx.adapters.get(session.agent_id);
835:   if (adapter === undefined || !isSessionCapableAdapter(adapter) || !adapter.sessionCapabilities().resume) {
836:     return singleErr(req.id, "resume_unsupported", "agent does not support resume");
837:   }
838:   if (ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)) {
839:     return singleErr(req.id, "session_busy", "session is already applying another instruction");
840:   }
841: 
842:   // Refuse busy/denied admission before minting an instruction receipt.
843:   let admission: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof acquireManualSessionAdmission>>;
844:   try { admission = await acquireManualSessionAdmission(ctx, session, p.message_id); }
845:   catch { return singleErr(req.id, "admission_denied", "Session execution admission was denied; no instruction was reserved"); }
846:   let handedOff = false;
847:   try {
848:     if (ctx.draining || admission.controller.signal.aborted) return singleErr(req.id, "admission_denied", "Session execution was cancelled; no instruction was reserved");
849:     let applied: { outcome: "applied" | "duplicate"; session_version: number };
850:     const now = Date.now() / 1000;
851:     try {
852:       applied = reserveSessionInstruction(ctx.db, ctx.dbPath, {
853:         message_id: p.message_id,
854:         session_id: p.session_id,
855:         turn_id: p.turn_id,
856:         direction: "inbound",
857:         kind: "instruction.send",
858:         payload: { text: p.text, ...(p.requested_by === undefined ? {} : { requested_by: p.requested_by }) },
859:         state: "accepted",
860:         created_at: now,
861:       }, p.expected_version, now);
862:     } catch (err) {
863:       if (err instanceof SessionVersionConflict) {
864:         return singleErr(req.id, "version_conflict", "session version changed; refresh before retrying");
865:       }
866:       if (err instanceof SessionMessageIdConflict) {
867:         return singleErr(req.id, "message_id_conflict", "message_id was already used for different content");
868:       }
869:       if (err instanceof Error && ["delivery_uncertain", "continuation_in_flight", "grant_owned", "signed_approval_required", "invalid_decision"].includes(err.message)) return singleErr(req.id, err.message, "Refresh the decision and use its authorized owner route");
870:       throw err;
871:     }
872:     if (applied.outcome === "duplicate") {
873:       return ctx.sessions.getMessage(p.message_id)?.state === "applied"
874:         ? singleOk(req.id, { state: "duplicate", session_version: applied.session_version })
875:         : singleErr(req.id, "delivery_uncertain", "A receipt appeared during admission; inspect it before retrying");
876:     }
877: 
878:     handedOff = true;
879:     return await executeReservedSessionTurn(ctx, req, p, session, adapter, applied.session_version, { admittedLease: admission.lease, controller: admission.controller });
880:   } finally { if (!handedOff) await admission.release(); }

884: /** Track queued manual work before awaiting admission so drain/cancel can stop it.
885:  * No durable receipt exists until admission succeeds; callers transfer both
886:  * ownership and the same cancellation signal to the reserved-turn executor.
887:  */
888: async function acquireManualSessionAdmission(
889:   ctx: DaemonContext,
890:   session: NonNullable<ReturnType<SessionRepository["getSession"]>>,
891:   pendingMessageId: string,
892: ) {
893:   const controller = new AbortController();
894:   ctx.activeSessionSends.set(session.session_id, pendingMessageId);
895:   ctx.activeRuns.set(session.run_id, controller);
896:   const clear = () => {
897:     if (ctx.activeSessionSends.get(session.session_id) === pendingMessageId) ctx.activeSessionSends.delete(session.session_id);
898:     if (ctx.activeRuns.get(session.run_id) === controller) ctx.activeRuns.delete(session.run_id);
899:   };
900:   try {
901:     const lease = await acquireSessionAdmission(ctx, session.agent_id, session.repo_path, session.run_id, controller.signal);
902:     return { lease, controller, release: async () => { try { await lease.release(); } finally { clear(); } } };
903:   } catch (error) {
904:     clear();
905:     throw error;
906:   }
907: }

909: /** Private port: only an already persisted reservation may invoke the runtime. */
910: async function executeReservedSessionTurn(
911:   ctx: DaemonContext,
912:   req: DaemonRequest,
913:   p: SessionSendParams,
914:   session: NonNullable<ReturnType<SessionRepository["getSession"]>>,
915:   adapter: SessionCapableAdapter,
916:   reservationVersion: number,
917:   options: {
918:     admittedLease?: ExecutionLease;
919:     controller?: AbortController;
920:     completed?: (turnId: string, version: number, now: number) => void;
921:     engineeringPermit?: Parameters<import("@osstack/core").EngineeringStore["consumeDispatchPermit"]>[0];
922:   } = {},
923: ): Promise<SingleResponse> {
924:   const applied = { session_version: reservationVersion };
925:   ctx.activeSessionSends.set(session.session_id,p.message_id);

930:   let started = false;
931:   let settled = false;
932:   try {
933:     // This guard stays active even when the feature flag is off. An extended
934:     // receipt can enter this helper only with the one-use CAS winner's permit.

936:     if (engineeringPhase) {

939:       catch { return singleErr(req.id, "dispatch_gate_required", "The dispatch permit is invalid or already consumed"); }
940:     }

947:         ctx.sessions.markMessageFailed(p.message_id);

952:           "the agent failed and the reservation could not be finalized; inspect the session before retrying",
953:         );
954:       }
955:       return singleErr(req.id, code, message);
956:     };
957:     if (session.native_session_id === null) return failReservedMessage("resume_unsupported", "session cannot resume");
958:     const context: Record<string, unknown> = {};
959:     if (session.repo_path !== "") context["repo_path"] = session.repo_path;
960:     if (p.requested_by !== undefined) context["requested_by"] = p.requested_by;
961:     const input: AgentSessionInput = {
962:       session_id: session.session_id,
963:       task_id: session.task_id,
964:       run_id: session.run_id,
965:       turn_id: p.turn_id,
966:       native_session_id: session.native_session_id,
967:       expected_version: applied.session_version,
968:       message_id: p.message_id,
969:       prompt: p.text,
970:       ...(Object.keys(context).length === 0 ? {} : { context }),
971:     };

973:     let assistantText = "";
974:     let decision: unknown = null;
975:     let terminalCompleted = false;
976:     try {
977:       started = true;
978:       for await (const rawEvent of boundedAdapterEvents(adapter.resumeSession(input, controller.signal), controller.signal)) {
979:         const event = AgentSessionEventSchema.parse(rawEvent);
980:         if (event.type === "native_session.started") {
981:           if (event.native_session_id !== session.native_session_id) {
982:             return failReservedMessage(
983:               "adapter_protocol",

985:             );
986:           }

988:         }

996:           if (event.state !== "completed") {

1002:             );
